from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import importlib.util
import json
import sqlite3
import subprocess
import sys
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from io import StringIO
from pathlib import Path
from types import SimpleNamespace
from typing import Any

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import URL, create_engine, text
from sqlalchemy.engine import Engine

from app import models  # noqa: F401
from app.database import Base
from app.invariant_catalog import INVARIANT_CATALOG
from app.migrations.mysql_baseline import create_mysql_baseline
from app.migrations.mysql_invariants import installed_mysql_invariants
from app.migrations.runner import MIGRATIONS
from app.mysql_importer import (
    InjectedImportStop,
    MySQLImportError,
    import_sqlite_snapshot_to_mysql,
    resume_mysql_import,
)
from app.snapshot_import_common import SnapshotImportError, verify_source_unchanged


def _integration_script() -> Any:
    script = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / "scripts" / "mysql-integration.py"
    specification = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(
        "labelsystem_mysql_integration_script",
        script,
    )
    assert specification is not None
    assert specification.loader is not None
    module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(specification)
    specification.loader.exec_module(module)
    return module


def test_mysql_importer_registers_all_models_in_a_fresh_process() -> None:
    completed = subprocess.run(
        [
            sys.executable,
            "-c",
            "from app.database import Base; import app.mysql_importer; "
            "print(len(Base.metadata.tables))",
        ],
        cwd=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1],
        check=False,
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
    )

    assert completed.returncode == 0, completed.stderr
    assert completed.stdout.strip() == "153"


def _source_snapshot(path: Path, *, user_count: int = 5) -> Path:
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{path}")
    try:
        Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
        users = Base.metadata.tables["users"]
        with engine.begin() as connection:
            connection.execute(
                users.insert(),
                [
                    {
                        "id": row_id,
                        "username": f"mysql-operator-{row_id}",
                        "password_hash": "fixture-hash",
                        "display_name": f"MySQL 运营 {row_id}",
                        "is_active": row_id % 2 == 1,
                        "is_admin": row_id == 1,
                        "role": "operator",
                        "permissions_json": '{"b":2,"a":1}',
                        "last_login_at": None,
                        "created_at": datetime(
                            2026,
                            8,
                            28,
                            row_id,
                            tzinfo=timezone.utc,
                        ),
                    }
                    for row_id in range(1, user_count + 1)
                ],
            )
    finally:
        engine.dispose()
    path.chmod(0o400)
    return path


def _sha256(path: Path) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()


def _create_import_target(mysql_engine: Engine) -> None:
    with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
        create_mysql_baseline(connection, install_invariants=False)


def _user_row(row_id: int, *, display_name: str | None = None) -> dict[str, object]:
    return {
        "id": row_id,
        "username": f"mysql-operator-{row_id}",
        "password_hash": "fixture-hash",
        "display_name": display_name or f"MySQL 运营 {row_id}",
        "is_active": row_id % 2 == 1,
        "is_admin": row_id == 1,
        "role": "operator",
        "permissions_json": '{"a":1,"b":2}',
        "last_login_at": None,
        "created_at": datetime(2026, 8, 28, row_id % 24),
    }


def test_mysql_import_resumes_and_finalizes_with_next_auto_increment(
    mysql_engine: Engine,
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    source = _source_snapshot(tmp_path / "source.db", user_count=5)
    source_hash = _sha256(source)
    source_stat = source.stat()
    checkpoint_dir = tmp_path / "checkpoints"
    _create_import_target(mysql_engine)

    with pytest.raises(InjectedImportStop, match="1"):
        import_sqlite_snapshot_to_mysql(
            source,
            mysql_engine,
            checkpoint_dir,
            batch_size=2,
            stop_after_batches=1,
        )

    with mysql_engine.connect() as connection:
        assert connection.scalar(text("SELECT count(*) FROM users")) == 2
        assert connection.scalar(text("SELECT @@FOREIGN_KEY_CHECKS")) == 1

    report = resume_mysql_import(
        source,
        mysql_engine,
        checkpoint_dir,
        batch_size=2,
    )

    assert report.resumed is True
    assert report.duplicate_rows == 0
    assert report.table_rows["users"] == 5
    assert report.completed_tables == 139
    assert _sha256(source) == source_hash
    assert source.stat() == source_stat
    with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
        imported = connection.execute(
            text(
                "SELECT id, permissions_json, is_active FROM users "
                "ORDER BY id"
            )
        ).mappings().all()
        assert [row["id"] for row in imported] == [1, 2, 3, 4, 5]
        assert imported[0]["permissions_json"] == '{"a":1,"b":2}'
        assert imported[0]["is_active"] == 1
        connection.execute(
            Base.metadata.tables["users"].insert(),
            {
                "username": "after-mysql-import",
                "password_hash": "fixture-hash",
                "display_name": "迁移后",
                "is_active": True,
                "is_admin": False,
                "role": "operator",
                "permissions_json": "[]",
            },
        )
        assert connection.scalar(
            text("SELECT id FROM users WHERE username='after-mysql-import'")
        ) == 6
        assert len(installed_mysql_invariants(connection)) == sum(
            contract.owner == "database_trigger"
            for contract in INVARIANT_CATALOG.values()
        )
        assert connection.scalar(
            text(
                "SELECT count(*) FROM information_schema.COLUMNS "
                "WHERE TABLE_SCHEMA=DATABASE() AND COLUMN_NAME LIKE 'g_uq_%'"
            )
        ) == 8


def test_mysql_resume_accepts_exact_row_committed_before_checkpoint(
    mysql_engine: Engine,
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    source = _source_snapshot(tmp_path / "source.db", user_count=4)
    checkpoint_dir = tmp_path / "checkpoints"
    _create_import_target(mysql_engine)
    with pytest.raises(InjectedImportStop):
        import_sqlite_snapshot_to_mysql(
            source,
            mysql_engine,
            checkpoint_dir,
            batch_size=2,
            stop_after_batches=1,
        )
    with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
        connection.execute(Base.metadata.tables["users"].insert(), _user_row(3))

    report = resume_mysql_import(
        source,
        mysql_engine,
        checkpoint_dir,
        batch_size=2,
    )

    assert report.duplicate_rows == 1
    with mysql_engine.connect() as connection:
        assert connection.scalar(text("SELECT count(*) FROM users")) == 4


def test_mysql_resume_rejects_same_primary_key_with_different_row(
    mysql_engine: Engine,
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    source = _source_snapshot(tmp_path / "source.db", user_count=4)
    checkpoint_dir = tmp_path / "checkpoints"
    _create_import_target(mysql_engine)
    with pytest.raises(InjectedImportStop):
        import_sqlite_snapshot_to_mysql(
            source,
            mysql_engine,
            checkpoint_dir,
            batch_size=2,
            stop_after_batches=1,
        )
    with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
        connection.execute(
            Base.metadata.tables["users"].insert(),
            _user_row(3, display_name="must-not-echo-conflicting-value"),
        )

    with pytest.raises(MySQLImportError, match="users") as captured:
        resume_mysql_import(
            source,
            mysql_engine,
            checkpoint_dir,
            batch_size=2,
        )

    assert "must-not-echo-conflicting-value" not in str(captured.value)


def test_mysql_import_rejects_a_nonempty_first_target(
    mysql_engine: Engine,
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    source = _source_snapshot(tmp_path / "source.db", user_count=1)
    _create_import_target(mysql_engine)
    with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
        connection.execute(Base.metadata.tables["users"].insert(), _user_row(99))

    with pytest.raises(MySQLImportError, match="非空"):
        import_sqlite_snapshot_to_mysql(
            source,
            mysql_engine,
            tmp_path / "checkpoints",
        )


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("column", "value", "message"),
    [
        ("permissions_json", "must-not-echo-{bad-json", "非法 JSON"),
        ("display_name", "must-not-echo\x00nul", "NUL"),
        ("is_active", 2, "非法布尔值"),
    ],
)
def test_mysql_import_rejects_malformed_source_values_without_echoing_them(
    mysql_engine: Engine,
    tmp_path: Path,
    column: str,
    value: object,
    message: str,
) -> None:
    source = _source_snapshot(tmp_path / "source.db", user_count=1)
    source.chmod(0o600)
    connection = sqlite3.connect(source)
    try:
        connection.execute("PRAGMA ignore_check_constraints = ON")
        connection.execute(
            f'UPDATE users SET "{column}"=? WHERE id=1',
            (value,),
        )
        connection.commit()
    finally:
        connection.close()
    source.chmod(0o400)
    _create_import_target(mysql_engine)

    with pytest.raises(MySQLImportError, match=message) as captured:
        import_sqlite_snapshot_to_mysql(
            source,
            mysql_engine,
            tmp_path / "checkpoints",
        )

    assert "must-not-echo" not in str(captured.value)


def test_mysql_checkpoint_is_bound_to_one_target_schema(
    mysql_engine: Engine,
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    source = _source_snapshot(tmp_path / "source.db", user_count=3)
    checkpoint_dir = tmp_path / "checkpoints"
    _create_import_target(mysql_engine)
    with pytest.raises(InjectedImportStop):
        import_sqlite_snapshot_to_mysql(
            source,
            mysql_engine,
            checkpoint_dir,
            batch_size=2,
            stop_after_batches=1,
        )
    checkpoint_path = checkpoint_dir / "sqlite-to-mysql-import.json"
    checkpoint = json.loads(checkpoint_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    checkpoint["target_schema"] = "another_schema"
    checkpoint_path.chmod(0o600)
    checkpoint_path.write_text(
        json.dumps(checkpoint, ensure_ascii=False),
        encoding="utf-8",
    )

    with pytest.raises(MySQLImportError, match="target_schema"):
        resume_mysql_import(source, mysql_engine, checkpoint_dir, batch_size=2)


def test_mysql_import_cli_uses_migration_role_and_emits_only_safe_report(
    tmp_path: Path,
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    module = _integration_script()
    source = tmp_path / "frozen.db"
    source.write_bytes(b"sqlite-fixture")
    accounts = tmp_path / "accounts.json"
    accounts.write_text("{}", encoding="utf-8")
    checkpoint_dir = tmp_path / "checkpoints"
    calls: dict[str, object] = {}

    def fake_url(
        accounts_file: Path,
        *,
        role: str,
        database: str,
    ) -> str:
        calls["accounts_file"] = accounts_file
        calls["role"] = role
        calls["database"] = database
        return "mysql+pymysql://migration:must-not-echo@localhost/labels"

    class FakeConnection:
        def __enter__(self) -> "FakeConnection":
            return self

        def __exit__(self, *_args: object) -> None:
            return None

    class FakeEngine:
        def connect(self) -> FakeConnection:
            return FakeConnection()

        def dispose(self) -> None:
            calls["disposed"] = True

    fake_engine = FakeEngine()
    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "mysql_database_url_from_references", fake_url)
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        module,
        "create_mysql_migration_engine",
        lambda url: calls.setdefault("url", url) and fake_engine,
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        module,
        "assert_mysql_runtime_contract",
        lambda connection: calls.setdefault("contract_connection", connection),
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        module,
        "import_sqlite_snapshot_to_mysql",
        lambda source_path, target, checkpoints, *, batch_size: (
            calls.update(
                {
                    "source": source_path,
                    "target": target,
                    "checkpoints": checkpoints,
                    "batch_size": batch_size,
                }
            )
            or SimpleNamespace(
                source=source_path.resolve(),
                source_sha256="a" * 64,
                inserted_rows=12,
                duplicate_rows=1,
                table_rows={"users": 12},
                completed_tables=107,
                resumed=True,
            )
        ),
    )
    output = StringIO()

    exit_code = module.main(
        [
            "import-snapshot",
            "--source",
            str(source),
            "--checkpoint-dir",
            str(checkpoint_dir),
            "--database",
            "labels_migration",
            "--accounts",
            str(accounts),
            "--batch-size",
            "250",
        ],
        output=output,
    )

    payload = json.loads(output.getvalue())
    assert exit_code == 0
    assert calls["accounts_file"] == accounts
    assert calls["role"] == "migration"
    assert calls["database"] == "labels_migration"
    assert calls["source"] == source
    assert calls["target"] is fake_engine
    assert calls["checkpoints"] == checkpoint_dir
    assert calls["batch_size"] == 250
    assert calls["disposed"] is True
    assert payload == {
        "command": "import-snapshot",
        "completed_tables": 107,
        "duplicate_rows": 1,
        "inserted_rows": 12,
        "resumed": True,
        "source": str(source.resolve()),
        "source_sha256": "a" * 64,
        "status": "ok",
        "table_rows": {"users": 12},
        "target_schema": "labels_migration",
    }
    assert "must-not-echo" not in output.getvalue()


def test_mysql_import_cli_accepts_private_runtime_dsn_file(
    tmp_path: Path,
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    module = _integration_script()
    source = tmp_path / "snapshot.sqlite3"
    source.write_bytes(b"sqlite-fixture")
    secret_file = tmp_path / "migration-database-url"
    secret_file.write_text(
        "mysql+pymysql://migration:must-not-echo@mysql/labels_migration\n",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    secret_file.chmod(0o600)
    calls: dict[str, object] = {}

    class FakeConnection:
        def __enter__(self) -> "FakeConnection":
            return self

        def __exit__(self, *_args: object) -> None:
            return None

    class FakeEngine:
        def connect(self) -> FakeConnection:
            return FakeConnection()

        def dispose(self) -> None:
            calls["disposed"] = True

    fake_engine = FakeEngine()
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        module,
        "create_mysql_migration_engine",
        lambda url: calls.setdefault("url", str(url)) and fake_engine,
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        module,
        "assert_mysql_runtime_contract",
        lambda _connection: None,
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        module,
        "import_sqlite_snapshot_to_mysql",
        lambda source_path, target, checkpoints, *, batch_size: SimpleNamespace(
            source=source_path.resolve(),
            source_sha256="b" * 64,
            inserted_rows=4,
            duplicate_rows=0,
            table_rows={"users": 4},
            completed_tables=119,
            resumed=False,
        ),
    )
    output = StringIO()

    exit_code = module.main(
        [
            "import-snapshot",
            "--source",
            str(source),
            "--checkpoint-dir",
            str(tmp_path / "checkpoints"),
            "--database",
            "labels_migration",
            "--database-url-file",
            str(secret_file),
        ],
        output=output,
    )

    assert exit_code == 0
    assert calls["url"] == (
        "mysql+pymysql://migration:***@mysql/labels_migration"
    )
    assert calls["disposed"] is True
    assert "must-not-echo" not in output.getvalue()


def test_prepare_import_target_creates_baseline_without_invariants(
    tmp_path: Path,
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    module = _integration_script()
    secret_file = tmp_path / "migration-database-url"
    secret_file.write_text(
        "mysql+pymysql://migration:must-not-echo@mysql/labels_migration",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    secret_file.chmod(0o600)
    calls: dict[str, object] = {}

    class FakeConnection:
        pass

    class FakeBegin:
        def __enter__(self) -> FakeConnection:
            connection = FakeConnection()
            calls["connection"] = connection
            return connection

        def __exit__(self, *_args: object) -> None:
            return None

    class FakeEngine:
        def begin(self) -> FakeBegin:
            return FakeBegin()

        def dispose(self) -> None:
            calls["disposed"] = True

    fake_engine = FakeEngine()
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        module,
        "create_mysql_migration_engine",
        lambda _url: fake_engine,
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        module,
        "assert_mysql_runtime_contract",
        lambda connection: calls.setdefault("contract", connection),
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        module,
        "assert_mysql_import_schema_empty",
        lambda connection: calls.setdefault("empty_schema", connection),
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        module,
        "create_mysql_baseline",
        lambda connection, *, install_invariants: calls.update(
            {
                "baseline": connection,
                "install_invariants": install_invariants,
            }
        ),
    )
    output = StringIO()

    exit_code = module.main(
        [
            "prepare-import-target",
            "--database",
            "labels_migration",
            "--database-url-file",
            str(secret_file),
        ],
        output=output,
    )

    assert exit_code == 0
    assert calls["contract"] is calls["connection"]
    assert calls["empty_schema"] is calls["connection"]
    assert calls["baseline"] is calls["connection"]
    assert calls["install_invariants"] is False
    assert calls["disposed"] is True
    assert json.loads(output.getvalue()) == {
        "command": "prepare-import-target",
        "status": "ok",
        "target_schema": "labels_migration",
    }
    assert "must-not-echo" not in output.getvalue()


def test_upgrade_existing_schema_uses_migration_identity_and_latest_version(
    tmp_path: Path,
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    module = _integration_script()
    secret_file = tmp_path / "migration-database-url"
    secret_file.write_text(
        "mysql+pymysql://migration:must-not-echo@mysql/labels_migration",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    secret_file.chmod(0o600)
    calls: dict[str, object] = {}

    class FakeConnection:
        pass

    class FakeBegin:
        def __enter__(self) -> FakeConnection:
            connection = FakeConnection()
            calls["connection"] = connection
            return connection

        def __exit__(self, *_args: object) -> None:
            return None

    class FakeEngine:
        def begin(self) -> FakeBegin:
            return FakeBegin()

        def dispose(self) -> None:
            calls["disposed"] = True

    monkeypatch.setattr(
        module,
        "create_mysql_migration_engine",
        lambda _url: FakeEngine(),
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        module,
        "assert_mysql_runtime_contract",
        lambda connection: calls.setdefault("contract", connection),
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        module,
        "upgrade_mysql_schema",
        lambda connection, *, expected: calls.update(
            {"upgraded": connection, "expected": expected}
        ),
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        module,
        "assert_mysql_schema_version",
        lambda connection, *, expected: calls.update(
            {"verified": connection, "verified_expected": expected}
        ),
    )
    output = StringIO()

    exit_code = module.main(
        [
            "upgrade-schema",
            "--database",
            "labels_migration",
            "--database-url-file",
            str(secret_file),
        ],
        output=output,
    )

    expected = max(item.version for item in module.MIGRATIONS)
    assert exit_code == 0
    assert calls["contract"] is calls["connection"]
    assert calls["upgraded"] is calls["connection"]
    assert calls["verified"] is calls["connection"]
    assert calls["expected"] == calls["verified_expected"] == expected
    assert calls["disposed"] is True
    assert json.loads(output.getvalue()) == {
        "command": "upgrade-schema",
        "migration": expected,
        "status": "ok",
        "target_schema": "labels_migration",
    }
    assert "must-not-echo" not in output.getvalue()


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("url", "error"),
    [
        ("sqlite:////tmp/not-mysql.db", "MySQL"),
        (
            "mysql+pymysql://migration:secret@mysql/wrong_schema",
            "database",
        ),
    ],
)
def test_private_runtime_dsn_file_rejects_wrong_target(
    tmp_path: Path,
    url: str,
    error: str,
) -> None:
    module = _integration_script()
    secret_file = tmp_path / "migration-database-url"
    secret_file.write_text(url, encoding="utf-8")
    secret_file.chmod(0o600)

    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match=error):
        module.database_url_from_private_file(
            secret_file,
            expected_database="labels_migration",
        )


def test_private_runtime_dsn_file_rejects_unsafe_schema_name(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    module = _integration_script()
    secret_file = tmp_path / "migration-database-url"
    secret_file.write_text(
        "mysql+pymysql://migration:secret@mysql/labels_migration",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    secret_file.chmod(0o600)

    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="database"):
        module.database_url_from_private_file(
            secret_file,
            expected_database="labels;drop",
        )


def test_mysql_migration_engine_installs_the_session_contract(
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    module = _integration_script()
    database_url = object()
    fake_engine = object()
    dbapi_connection = object()
    calls: dict[str, object] = {}

    monkeypatch.setattr(
        module,
        "engine_options",
        lambda value: calls.setdefault("options_url", value)
        and {"pool_pre_ping": True},
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        module,
        "create_engine",
        lambda value, **options: calls.update(
            {"create_url": value, "create_options": options}
        )
        or fake_engine,
    )

    def fake_listen(target: object, event_name: str, listener: object) -> None:
        calls["listen_target"] = target
        calls["event_name"] = event_name
        calls["listener"] = listener

    monkeypatch.setattr(module.event, "listen", fake_listen)
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        module,
        "configure_mysql_dbapi_connection",
        lambda connection: calls.setdefault("configured", connection),
    )

    result = module.create_mysql_migration_engine(database_url)

    assert result is fake_engine
    assert calls["options_url"] is database_url
    assert calls["create_url"] is database_url
    assert calls["create_options"] == {"pool_pre_ping": True}
    assert calls["listen_target"] is fake_engine
    assert calls["event_name"] == "connect"
    listener = calls["listener"]
    assert callable(listener)
    listener(dbapi_connection, object())
    assert calls["configured"] is dbapi_connection


def test_source_verification_reports_removed_snapshot_as_import_error(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    source = tmp_path / "removed-during-import.db"
    source.write_bytes(b"frozen")
    source_stat = source.stat()
    source_hash = _sha256(source)
    source.unlink()

    with pytest.raises(SnapshotImportError, match="无法复核"):
        verify_source_unchanged(
            source,
            initial_stat=source_stat,
            initial_sha256=source_hash,
        )


def test_clone_schema_cli_uses_root_maintenance_and_emits_safe_report(
    tmp_path: Path,
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    module = _integration_script()
    accounts_file = tmp_path / "accounts.json"
    accounts_file.write_text("{}", encoding="utf-8")
    private_runtime = tmp_path / "private-runtime"
    calls: dict[str, object] = {"roles": []}

    def fake_url(
        _accounts_file: Path,
        *,
        role: str,
        database: str | None,
    ) -> URL:
        roles = calls["roles"]
        assert isinstance(roles, list)
        roles.append((role, database))
        if role == "root":
            return URL.create(
                "mysql+pymysql",
                username="root",
                password="root-fixture-secret",
                host="localhost",
                port=33084,
                query={"unix_socket": "/tmp/mysql.sock", "charset": "utf8mb4"},
            )
        return URL.create(
            "mysql+pymysql",
            username="source-migration",
            password="source-fixture-secret",
            host="localhost",
            port=33084,
            database=database,
            query={"unix_socket": "/tmp/mysql.sock", "charset": "utf8mb4"},
        )

    class Scope:
        def __init__(self, connection: object) -> None:
            self.connection = connection

        def __enter__(self) -> object:
            return self.connection

        def __exit__(self, *_args: object) -> None:
            return None

    root_connection = object()
    target_connection = object()

    class FakeEngine:
        def __init__(self, connection: object) -> None:
            self.connection = connection

        def begin(self) -> Scope:
            return Scope(self.connection)

        def connect(self) -> Scope:
            return Scope(self.connection)

        def dispose(self) -> None:
            calls.setdefault("disposed", []).append(self.connection)

    root_engine = FakeEngine(root_connection)
    target_engine = FakeEngine(target_connection)

    def fake_engine(url: URL) -> FakeEngine:
        return root_engine if url.username == "root" else target_engine

    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "mysql_database_url_from_references", fake_url)
    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "create_mysql_migration_engine", fake_engine)
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        module,
        "prepare_candidate_database",
        lambda connection, **kwargs: calls.update(
            {"prepared_connection": connection, "prepared": kwargs}
        ),
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        module,
        "clone_schema_with_clients",
        lambda **kwargs: calls.update({"cloned": kwargs})
        or SimpleNamespace(
            source_schema=kwargs["source_schema"],
            target_schema=kwargs["target_schema"],
            stripped_definers=44,
        ),
    )
    evidence = SimpleNamespace(
        migration=87,
        tables={"assets": object(), "workflow_versions": object()},
        views=(),
        trigger_count=0,
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "mysql_schema_evidence", lambda *_args: evidence)
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        module,
        "assert_exact_schema_clone",
        lambda *_args: SimpleNamespace(
            table_count=2,
            row_count=5,
            evidence_hash="a" * 64,
        ),
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        module,
        "upgrade_mysql_schema",
        lambda connection, *, expected: calls.update(
            {"upgrade_connection": connection, "upgrade_expected": expected}
        ),
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        module,
        "assert_mysql_schema_version",
        lambda connection, *, expected: calls.update(
            {"assert_connection": connection, "assert_expected": expected}
        ),
    )
    app_file = private_runtime / "app-database-url"
    migration_file = private_runtime / "migration-database-url"

    def fake_write(private_root: Path, **_kwargs: object) -> object:
        private_root.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        app_file.write_text("private", encoding="utf-8")
        migration_file.write_text("private", encoding="utf-8")
        return SimpleNamespace(app=app_file, migration=migration_file)

    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "write_candidate_runtime_dsns", fake_write)
    monkeypatch.setattr(module.secrets, "token_urlsafe", lambda _size: "generated-secret")
    output = StringIO()

    exit_code = module.main(
        [
            "clone-schema",
            "--source",
            "labelsystem_integration_candidate_20260829_f3b95eb",
            "--target",
            "labelsystem_docker_candidate_20260829_04c8298",
            "--accounts",
            str(accounts_file),
            "--mysql-prefix",
            str(tmp_path / "mysql84"),
            "--private-runtime-dir",
            str(private_runtime),
        ],
        output=output,
    )

    payload = json.loads(output.getvalue())
    assert exit_code == 0
    assert calls["roles"] == [
        ("root", None),
        ("migration", "labelsystem_integration_candidate_20260829_f3b95eb"),
    ]
    assert calls["prepared_connection"] is root_connection
    assert calls["upgrade_connection"] is target_connection
    assert payload == {
        "app_database_url_file": str(app_file.resolve()),
        "clone_evidence_hash": "a" * 64,
        "cloned_migration": 87,
        "cloned_rows": 5,
        "cloned_tables": 2,
        "command": "clone-schema",
        "migration_database_url_file": str(migration_file.resolve()),
        "source_schema": "labelsystem_integration_candidate_20260829_f3b95eb",
        "status": "ok",
        "stripped_definers": 44,
        "target_migration": max(item.version for item in MIGRATIONS),
        "target_schema": "labelsystem_docker_candidate_20260829_04c8298",
    }
    assert "fixture-secret" not in output.getvalue()
    assert "generated-secret" not in output.getvalue()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_mysql_integration.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import importlib
import json
import os
import stat
from io import StringIO
from pathlib import Path
from subprocess import CompletedProcess
from types import SimpleNamespace
from typing import Any, Sequence

import pytest

from app import security


def _module() -> Any:
    try:
        return importlib.import_module("app.mysql_integration")
    except ModuleNotFoundError:
        pytest.fail("app.mysql_integration 尚未实现", pytrace=False)


def _integration_root(tmp_path: Path) -> tuple[Path, Path]:
    volume = tmp_path / "WorkSSD"
    root = volume / "3d66-label-system-integration" / "mysql84"
    root.mkdir(parents=True)
    return volume, root


def _prepare_safe_module(
    tmp_path: Path,
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> tuple[Any, Path]:
    module = _module()
    volume, root = _integration_root(tmp_path)
    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "INTEGRATION_VOLUME_ROOT", volume)
    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "MYSQL_INTEGRATION_ROOT", root)
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        module.shutil,
        "disk_usage",
        lambda _path: SimpleNamespace(
            total=500 * 1024**3,
            used=200 * 1024**3,
            free=300 * 1024**3,
        ),
    )
    return module, root


def _fake_binaries(module: Any, tmp_path: Path) -> Any:
    prefix = tmp_path / "mysql84"
    binary_dir = prefix / "bin"
    binary_dir.mkdir(parents=True)
    for name in ("mysqld", "mysql", "mysqladmin"):
        path = binary_dir / name
        path.write_text("#!/bin/sh\nexit 0\n", encoding="utf-8")
        path.chmod(0o755)
    return module.MySQLBinaries.for_prefix(prefix)


def test_mysql_integration_paths_are_confined_to_mysql84_directory(
    tmp_path: Path,
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    module, root = _prepare_safe_module(tmp_path, monkeypatch)

    paths = module.MySQLIntegrationPaths.from_root(root)
    paths.assert_safe()

    assert paths.root == root
    assert paths.datadir == root / "data"
    assert paths.socket_dir == root / "socket"
    assert paths.socket == root / "socket" / "mysql.sock"
    assert paths.logs == root / "logs"
    assert paths.checkpoints == root / "checkpoints"
    assert paths.reports == root / "reports"
    assert paths.config == root / "my.cnf"
    assert paths.pid_file == root / "mysql.pid"
    assert paths.accounts_file == root / "accounts.json"
    assert paths.port == 33084


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "unsafe_suffix",
    [
        (),
        ("3d66-label-system-integration",),
        ("unrelated-project", "mysql84"),
    ],
)
def test_mysql_integration_paths_reject_broad_or_wrong_roots(
    tmp_path: Path,
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
    unsafe_suffix: tuple[str, ...],
) -> None:
    module, root = _prepare_safe_module(tmp_path, monkeypatch)
    volume = root.parents[1]
    unsafe = volume.joinpath(*unsafe_suffix)

    with pytest.raises(module.IntegrationPathError, match="MySQL 8.4"):
        module.MySQLIntegrationPaths.from_root(unsafe).assert_safe()


def test_mysql_integration_paths_reject_symlinked_child(
    tmp_path: Path,
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    module, root = _prepare_safe_module(tmp_path, monkeypatch)
    outside = tmp_path / "outside"
    outside.mkdir()
    (root / "logs").symlink_to(outside, target_is_directory=True)

    with pytest.raises(module.IntegrationPathError, match="符号链接"):
        module.MySQLIntegrationPaths.from_root(root).assert_safe()


def test_mysql_disk_report_enforces_project_and_free_space_limits(
    tmp_path: Path,
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    module, root = _prepare_safe_module(tmp_path, monkeypatch)
    (root / "small.bin").write_bytes(b"1234567")

    report = module.integration_disk_report(
        module.MySQLIntegrationPaths.from_root(root)
    )

    assert report == {
        "root": str(root.resolve()),
        "project_used_bytes": 7,
        "project_limit_bytes": 120 * 1024**3,
        "filesystem_total_bytes": 500 * 1024**3,
        "filesystem_free_bytes": 300 * 1024**3,
        "minimum_free_bytes": 40 * 1024**3,
    }


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("project_bytes", "free_bytes", "message"),
    [
        (120 * 1024**3, 300 * 1024**3, "120 GiB"),
        (1, 40 * 1024**3 - 1, "40 GiB"),
    ],
)
def test_mysql_disk_report_fails_closed_at_capacity_limits(
    tmp_path: Path,
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
    project_bytes: int,
    free_bytes: int,
    message: str,
) -> None:
    module, root = _prepare_safe_module(tmp_path, monkeypatch)
    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "_directory_size", lambda _root: project_bytes)
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        module.shutil,
        "disk_usage",
        lambda _path: SimpleNamespace(
            total=500 * 1024**3,
            used=500 * 1024**3 - free_bytes,
            free=free_bytes,
        ),
    )

    with pytest.raises(module.IntegrationCapacityError, match=message):
        module.integration_disk_report(
            module.MySQLIntegrationPaths.from_root(root)
        )


def test_initialize_mysql_writes_private_local_only_configuration(
    tmp_path: Path,
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    module, root = _prepare_safe_module(tmp_path, monkeypatch)
    binaries = _fake_binaries(module, tmp_path)
    commands: list[tuple[str, ...]] = []

    def fake_run(command: Sequence[str]) -> CompletedProcess[str]:
        commands.append(tuple(command))
        if tuple(command[1:]) == ("--version",):
            return CompletedProcess(
                command,
                0,
                "mysqld  Ver 8.4.11 for macos15.0 on arm64 (Homebrew)",
                "",
            )
        if "--initialize-insecure" in command:
            (root / "data" / "auto.cnf").write_text(
                "[auto]\nserver-uuid=fixed-test-uuid\n",
                encoding="utf-8",
            )
        return CompletedProcess(command, 0, "", "")

    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "_port_available", lambda _port: True)
    paths = module.MySQLIntegrationPaths.from_root(root)
    module.initialize_mysql(paths, binaries, run_command=fake_run)

    assert len(commands) == 2
    assert commands[0] == (str(binaries.mysqld), "--version")
    assert commands[1][0] == str(binaries.mysqld)
    assert f"--defaults-file={paths.config}" in commands[1]
    assert "--initialize-insecure" in commands[1]
    rendered = paths.config.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    assert "bind-address=127.0.0.1" in rendered
    assert "port=33084" in rendered
    assert f"socket={paths.socket}" in rendered
    assert "mysqlx=0" in rendered
    assert "default-storage-engine=InnoDB" in rendered
    assert "transaction-isolation=READ-COMMITTED" in rendered
    assert "character-set-server=utf8mb4" in rendered
    assert "collation-server=utf8mb4_0900_as_cs" in rendered
    assert "default-time-zone=+00:00" in rendered
    assert "local-infile=0" in rendered
    assert "symbolic-links=0" in rendered
    assert "log-bin-trust-function-creators=ON" in rendered
    assert "binlog-expire-logs-auto-purge=ON" in rendered
    assert "binlog-expire-logs-seconds=86400" in rendered
    assert paths.config.stat().st_mode & 0o777 == 0o600
    assert paths.root.stat().st_mode & 0o777 == 0o700
    marker = json.loads(paths.instance_marker.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    assert marker["format"] == "labelsystem-mysql84-instance-v1"
    assert marker["port"] == 33084
    assert marker["version"] == "8.4.11"

def test_start_and_stop_use_exact_config_and_validated_pid(
    tmp_path: Path,
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    module, root = _prepare_safe_module(tmp_path, monkeypatch)
    paths = module.MySQLIntegrationPaths.from_root(root)
    paths.datadir.mkdir()
    paths.config.write_text("[mysqld]\n", encoding="utf-8")
    paths.instance_marker.write_text(
        json.dumps(
            {
                "format": "labelsystem-mysql84-instance-v1",
                "root": str(root.resolve()),
                "port": 33084,
                "version": "8.4.11",
            }
        ),
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    binaries = _fake_binaries(module, tmp_path)
    started: list[tuple[str, ...]] = []

    class FakeProcess:
        pid = 43210

    def fake_start(command: Sequence[str], **_kwargs: object) -> FakeProcess:
        started.append(tuple(command))
        paths.pid_file.write_text("43210\n", encoding="ascii")
        return FakeProcess()

    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "_wait_for_mysql_ready", lambda *_args, **_kwargs: None)
    module.start_mysql(paths, binaries, start_process=fake_start)

    assert started == [
        (
            str(binaries.mysqld),
            f"--defaults-file={paths.config}",
            "--daemonize",
        )
    ]
    terminated: list[int] = []
    module.stop_mysql(
        paths,
        process_command=lambda _pid: (
            f"{binaries.mysqld} --defaults-file={paths.config} --daemonize"
        ),
        terminate_pid=terminated.append,
    )
    assert terminated == [43210]


def test_stop_mysql_refuses_stale_pid_for_another_process(
    tmp_path: Path,
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    module, root = _prepare_safe_module(tmp_path, monkeypatch)
    paths = module.MySQLIntegrationPaths.from_root(root)
    paths.datadir.mkdir()
    paths.config.write_text("[mysqld]\n", encoding="utf-8")

    paths.instance_marker.write_text(
        json.dumps(
            {
                "format": "labelsystem-mysql84-instance-v1",
                "root": str(root.resolve()),
                "port": 33084,
                "version": "8.4.11",
            }
        ),
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    terminated: list[int] = []

    with pytest.raises(module.MySQLIntegrationError, match="不属于"):
        module.stop_mysql(
            paths,
            process_command=lambda _pid: "/usr/bin/python unrelated.py",
            terminate_pid=terminated.append,
        )

    assert terminated == []

class _SQLRecorder:
    dialect = SimpleNamespace(name="mysql")

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.calls: list[tuple[str, object]] = []

    def exec_driver_sql(
        self,
        statement: str,
        parameters: object = None,
    ) -> _ScalarResult:
        # SQLAlchemy hands PyMySQL an empty mapping for parameterless driver SQL;
        # PyMySQL still applies percent interpolation in that case.
        if parameters is None:
            statement = statement % {}
        self.calls.append((statement, parameters))
        return _ScalarResult(None)


class _ScalarResult:
    def __init__(self, value: object):
        self.value = value

    def scalar_one(self) -> object:
        return self.value


def test_bootstrap_accounts_store_only_keychain_references(
    tmp_path: Path,
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    module, root = _prepare_safe_module(tmp_path, monkeypatch)
    paths = module.MySQLIntegrationPaths.from_root(root)
    recorder = _SQLRecorder()
    secrets = iter(("root-secret", "migration-secret", "app-secret", "test-secret"))
    protected: dict[str, str] = {}

    def protect(value: str, *, account: str) -> str:
        protected[account] = value
        return f"keychain:v1:{account}"

    references = module.bootstrap_mysql_accounts(
        recorder,  # type: ignore[arg-type]
        paths,
        protect_secret_fn=protect,
        password_factory=lambda: next(secrets),
    )

    rendered_sql = "\n".join(statement for statement, _params in recorder.calls)
    rendered_parameters = repr([params for _statement, params in recorder.calls])
    assert "root-secret" not in rendered_sql
    assert "migration-secret" not in rendered_sql
    assert "app-secret" not in rendered_sql
    assert "test-secret" not in rendered_sql
    assert "root-secret" in rendered_parameters
    assert " ON *.* " not in rendered_sql
    assert "`labelsystem\\_integration\\_%`.*" in rendered_sql
    assert "`labelsystem\\_test\\_%`.*" in rendered_sql
    assert references.migration_password_reference.startswith("keychain:v1:")
    payload = paths.accounts_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    assert "root-secret" not in payload
    assert "migration-secret" not in payload
    assert "app-secret" not in payload
    assert "test-secret" not in payload
    assert '"format": "labelsystem-mysql84-accounts-v1"' in payload
    assert paths.accounts_file.stat().st_mode & 0o777 == (
        stat.S_IRUSR | stat.S_IWUSR
    )


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "account",
    (
        "mysql84-root",
        "mysql84-migration",
        "mysql84-app",
        "mysql84-test-admin",
    ),
)
def test_mysql_account_names_are_supported_by_real_secret_storage(
    account: str,
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    stored: dict[str, str] = {}

    class FakeKeychain:
        def set_secret(self, key: str, value: str) -> None:
            stored[key] = value

    monkeypatch.setattr(security.sys, "platform", "darwin")
    monkeypatch.setattr(security, "_get_macos_keychain", FakeKeychain)

    reference = security.protect_secret("fixed-test-secret", account=account)

    assert reference == f"keychain:v1:{account}"
    assert stored == {account: "fixed-test-secret"}


def test_database_url_resolves_secret_in_memory_without_changing_accounts_file(
    tmp_path: Path,
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    module, root = _prepare_safe_module(tmp_path, monkeypatch)
    paths = module.MySQLIntegrationPaths.from_root(root)
    paths.accounts_file.write_text(
        json.dumps(
            {
                "format": "labelsystem-mysql84-accounts-v1",
                "socket": str(paths.socket),
                "port": 33084,
                "migration_user": "labelsystem_migration",
                "migration_password_reference": "keychain:v1:migration",
                "app_user": "labelsystem_app",
                "app_password_reference": "keychain:v1:app",
                "test_user": "labelsystem_test_admin",
                "test_password_reference": "keychain:v1:test",
            }
        ),
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    paths.accounts_file.chmod(0o600)
    original = paths.accounts_file.read_bytes()
    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "unprotect_secret", lambda _reference: "resolved-secret")

    url = module.mysql_database_url_from_references(
        paths.accounts_file,
        role="migration",
        database="mysql",
    )

    assert url.drivername == "mysql+pymysql"
    assert url.username == "labelsystem_migration"
    assert url.password == "resolved-secret"
    assert url.database == "mysql"
    assert url.query["unix_socket"] == str(paths.socket)
    assert paths.accounts_file.read_bytes() == original

    assert url.username == "labelsystem_test_admin"


def test_database_url_supports_root_maintenance_without_exposing_secret(
    tmp_path: Path,
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    module, root = _prepare_safe_module(tmp_path, monkeypatch)

    assert url.password == "must-not-be-rendered"
    assert "must-not-be-rendered" not in str(url)


def test_accounts_loader_rejects_symlink(
    tmp_path: Path,
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    module, root = _prepare_safe_module(tmp_path, monkeypatch)
    paths = module.MySQLIntegrationPaths.from_root(root)
    outside = tmp_path / "accounts.json"
    outside.write_text(
        json.dumps(
            {
                "format": "labelsystem-mysql84-accounts-v1",
                "password": "plaintext-is-forbidden",
            }
        ),
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    paths.accounts_file.symlink_to(outside)

    with pytest.raises(module.MySQLIntegrationError, match="普通文件"):
        module.mysql_database_url_from_references(
            paths.accounts_file,
            role="migration",
            database="mysql",
        )

        )

            database="mysql",

from __future__ import annotations

    MYSQL_PARTIAL_UNIQUE_SPECS,
    install_mysql_invariants,
    installed_mysql_invariants,
)


_NOW = datetime(2026, 8, 28, 0, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)


def _partial_unique_row(table_name: str, **overrides: object) -> dict[str, object]:
    rows: dict[str, dict[str, object]] = {
        "category_evaluation_v3_revisions": {
            "category_key": "partial-category",
            "display_name": "条件唯一类目",
            "revision": 1,
            "status": "active",
            "contract_json": "{}",
            "classification_map_json": "{}",
            "subcategory_dimensions_json": "{}",
            "contract_hash": "a" * 64,
        },
        "evaluation_jobs": {
            "asset_id": 9001,
            "regression_item_id": 9101,
            "baseline_regression_item_id": None,
            "strategy_bundle_id": 9201,
            "technical_attempt": 0,
        },
        "source_identity_verifications": {
            "contract_key": "partial-source-contract",
            "source_system": "partial-source",
            "key_fields_json": "[]",
            "result": "verified",
            "probe_hash": "b" * 64,
            "data_window": "2026-08-28",
            "scoped_row_count": 1,
            "duplicate_key_count": 0,
            "res_id_conflict_count": 0,
            "status": "approved",
            "created_by": "mysql-invariant-test",
        },
        "content_records": {
            "source_system": "partial-content-source",
            "source_content_id": "partial-content-1",
            "category_key": "partial-category",
            "source_version": "v1",
            "source_occurred_at": _NOW,
            "content_key": "partial-content-key",
            "identity_status": "verified",
            "status": "ready",
        },
        "tag_demand_contracts": {
            "contract_key": "partial-tag-contract",
            "version": 1,
            "status": "active",
            "definition_json": "{}",
            "contract_hash": "c" * 64,
        },
        "semantic_tag_facts": {
            "asset_version_id": 9301,
            "field_key": "partial-field",
            "fact_version": 1,
            "field_status": "required",
            "values_json": "[]",
            "evidence_json": "[]",
            "source_evaluation_id": 9401,
            "source_review_id": 9501,
            "contract_id": 9601,
            "normalization_version": "v1",
            "mapping_version": "v1",
            "status": "approved",
            "payload_hash": "d" * 64,
        },
        "baseline_truth_revisions": {
            "baseline_set_item_id": 9701,
            "revision": 1,
            "truth_kind": "structured_fields",
            "expected_level": None,
            "expected_result_json": "{}",
            "truth_contract_key": "partial-truth-contract",
            "workflow_input_json": "{}",
            "source_correction_key": "partial-correction-key",
            "reason": "验证条件唯一约束",
            "actor": "mysql-invariant-test",
            "previous_hash": "0" * 64,
            "canonical_hash": "e" * 64,
        },
    }
    row = dict(rows[table_name])
    row.update(overrides)
    return row


def _insert_core_row(connection, table_name: str, **values: object) -> None:
    connection.execute(
        Base.metadata.tables[table_name].insert(),
        _partial_unique_row(table_name, **values),
    )


def _assert_insert_rejected(connection, table_name: str, **values: object) -> None:
    transaction = connection.begin()
    try:
        with pytest.raises(IntegrityError):
            _insert_core_row(connection, table_name, **values)
    finally:
        transaction.rollback()


def _insert_and_commit(connection, table_name: str, **values: object) -> None:
    with connection.begin():
        _insert_core_row(connection, table_name, **values)


def _final_sqlite_trigger_names() -> set[str]:
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(
        prefix="labelsystem-mysql-invariant-inventory-"
    ) as temporary:
        database = Path(temporary) / "inventory.db"
        engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{database}")
        try:
            Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
            with engine.begin() as connection:
                run_migrations(connection)
            with engine.connect() as connection:
                return {
                    str(row[0])
                    for row in connection.execute(
                        text(
                            "SELECT name FROM sqlite_master "
                            "WHERE type='trigger'"
                        )
                    )
                }
        finally:
            engine.dispose()


def test_invariant_catalog_covers_every_final_sqlite_trigger() -> None:
    sqlite_triggers = _final_sqlite_trigger_names()

    assert len(INVARIANT_CATALOG) == 117
    assert set(INVARIANT_CATALOG) == sqlite_triggers
    assert Counter(
        contract.owner for contract in INVARIANT_CATALOG.values()
    ) == {
        "database_trigger": 48,
        "orm_guard": 69,
    }
    assert INVARIANT_CATALOG[
        "trg_workflow_foreach_items_identity_frozen"
    ].owner == "database_trigger"
    assert INVARIANT_CATALOG[
        "trg_workflow_foreach_items_no_delete"
    ].owner == "database_trigger"
    assert all(contract.rationale.strip() for contract in INVARIANT_CATALOG.values())
    database_contracts = {
        name: contract
        for name, contract in INVARIANT_CATALOG.items()
        if contract.owner == "database_trigger"
    }
    assert all(contract.table_name for contract in database_contracts.values())
    assert all(
        contract.event in {"UPDATE", "DELETE"}
        for contract in database_contracts.values()
    )
    assert all(contract.message for contract in database_contracts.values())
    assert INVARIANT_CATALOG[
        "trg_workflow_model_invocations_identity_frozen"
    ].owner == "database_trigger"
    assert INVARIANT_CATALOG[
        "trg_evaluation_packages_state_transition"
    ].owner == "orm_guard"


def test_mysql_partial_unique_specs_freeze_all_eight_contracts() -> None:
    actual = {
        (
            spec.table_name,
            spec.index_name,
            spec.key_columns,
            spec.predicate,
        )
        for spec in MYSQL_PARTIAL_UNIQUE_SPECS
    }

    assert actual == {
        (
            "category_evaluation_v3_revisions",
            "uq_category_evaluation_v3_revisions_active",
            ("category_key",),
            "status = 'active'",
        ),
        (
            "evaluation_jobs",
            "uq_evaluation_jobs_regression_strategy",
            ("regression_item_id", "strategy_bundle_id"),
            "regression_item_id IS NOT NULL AND strategy_bundle_id IS NOT NULL "
            "AND technical_attempt = 0",
        ),
        (
            "evaluation_jobs",
            "uq_evaluation_jobs_baseline_regression_item",
            ("baseline_regression_item_id",),
            "baseline_regression_item_id IS NOT NULL AND technical_attempt = 0",
        ),
        (
            "source_identity_verifications",
            "uq_source_identity_verifications_approved_source",
            ("contract_key", "source_system"),
            "status = 'approved'",
        ),
        (
            "content_records",
            "uq_content_records_verified_key",
            ("content_key",),
            "content_key IS NOT NULL",
        ),
        (
            "tag_demand_contracts",
            "uq_tag_demand_contracts_active_key",
            ("contract_key",),
            "status = 'active'",
        ),
        (
            "semantic_tag_facts",
            "uq_semantic_tag_fact_review_approval",
            (
                "source_evaluation_id",
                "source_review_id",
                "asset_version_id",
                "field_key",
            ),
            "status = 'approved'",
        ),
        (
            "baseline_truth_revisions",
            "uq_baseline_truth_revisions_correction",
            ("source_correction_key",),
            "source_correction_key IS NOT NULL",
        ),
    }
    assert len(MYSQL_PARTIAL_UNIQUE_SPECS) == 8


def test_mysql_partial_unique_guards_install_as_generated_unique_indexes(
    mysql_engine: Engine,
) -> None:
    with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
        create_mysql_baseline(connection, install_invariants=False)
        install_mysql_invariants(connection)
        generated = {
            (str(row.TABLE_NAME), str(row.COLUMN_NAME)): (
                str(row.GENERATION_EXPRESSION),
                str(row.EXTRA),
            )
            for row in connection.execute(
                text(
                    "SELECT TABLE_NAME, COLUMN_NAME, GENERATION_EXPRESSION, EXTRA "
                    "FROM information_schema.COLUMNS "
                    "WHERE TABLE_SCHEMA = DATABASE() "
                    "AND COLUMN_NAME LIKE 'g_uq_%'"
                )
            )
        }
        indexes = {
            str(row.INDEX_NAME): (
                str(row.TABLE_NAME),
                tuple(
                    str(column.COLUMN_NAME)
                    for column in connection.execute(
                        text(
                            "SELECT COLUMN_NAME FROM information_schema.STATISTICS "
                            "WHERE TABLE_SCHEMA = DATABASE() "
                            "AND TABLE_NAME = :table_name "
                            "AND INDEX_NAME = :index_name "
                            "ORDER BY SEQ_IN_INDEX"
                        ),
                        {
                            "table_name": str(row.TABLE_NAME),
                            "index_name": str(row.INDEX_NAME),
                        },
                    )
                ),
            )
            for row in connection.execute(
                text(
                    "SELECT DISTINCT TABLE_NAME, INDEX_NAME "
                    "FROM information_schema.STATISTICS "
                    "WHERE TABLE_SCHEMA = DATABASE() AND NON_UNIQUE = 0 "
                    "AND INDEX_NAME LIKE 'uq_%'"
                )
            )
        }

    for spec in MYSQL_PARTIAL_UNIQUE_SPECS:
        expression, extra = generated[(spec.table_name, spec.guard_column)]
        assert "case" in expression.lower()
        assert "virtual generated" in extra.lower()
        assert indexes[spec.index_name] == (
            spec.table_name,
            (*spec.key_columns, spec.guard_column),
        )


def test_mysql_partial_unique_guards_enforce_all_eight_contracts(
    mysql_engine: Engine,
) -> None:
    with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
        create_mysql_baseline(connection, install_invariants=False)
        install_mysql_invariants(connection)

    with mysql_engine.connect() as connection:
        connection.exec_driver_sql("SET SESSION FOREIGN_KEY_CHECKS = 0")
        connection.commit()
        try:
            _insert_and_commit(connection, "category_evaluation_v3_revisions")
            _assert_insert_rejected(
                connection,
                "category_evaluation_v3_revisions",
                revision=2,
                contract_hash="f" * 64,
            )
            _insert_and_commit(
                connection,
                "category_evaluation_v3_revisions",
                revision=3,
                status="candidate",
                contract_hash="1" * 64,
            )
            _insert_and_commit(
                connection,
                "category_evaluation_v3_revisions",
                category_key="partial-category-other",
                revision=1,
                contract_hash="2" * 64,
            )

            _insert_and_commit(connection, "evaluation_jobs")
            _assert_insert_rejected(
                connection,
                "evaluation_jobs",
                asset_id=9002,
            )
            _insert_and_commit(
                connection,
                "evaluation_jobs",
                asset_id=9003,
                technical_attempt=1,
            )
            _insert_and_commit(
                connection,
                "evaluation_jobs",
                asset_id=9004,
                strategy_bundle_id=9202,
            )

            _insert_and_commit(
                connection,
                "evaluation_jobs",
                asset_id=9005,
                regression_item_id=None,
                strategy_bundle_id=None,
                baseline_regression_item_id=9801,
            )
            _assert_insert_rejected(
                connection,
                "evaluation_jobs",
                asset_id=9006,
                regression_item_id=None,
                strategy_bundle_id=None,
                baseline_regression_item_id=9801,
            )
            _insert_and_commit(
                connection,
                "evaluation_jobs",
                asset_id=9007,
                regression_item_id=None,
                strategy_bundle_id=None,
                baseline_regression_item_id=9801,
                technical_attempt=1,
            )
            _insert_and_commit(
                connection,
                "evaluation_jobs",
                asset_id=9008,
                regression_item_id=None,
                strategy_bundle_id=None,
                baseline_regression_item_id=9802,
            )

            _insert_and_commit(connection, "source_identity_verifications")
            _assert_insert_rejected(
                connection,
                "source_identity_verifications",
                probe_hash="3" * 64,
            )
            _insert_and_commit(
                connection,
                "source_identity_verifications",
                probe_hash="4" * 64,
                status="draft",
            )
            _insert_and_commit(
                connection,
                "source_identity_verifications",
                source_system="partial-source-other",
                probe_hash="5" * 64,
            )

            _insert_and_commit(connection, "content_records")
            _assert_insert_rejected(
                connection,
                "content_records",
                source_content_id="partial-content-2",
            )
            _insert_and_commit(
                connection,
                "content_records",
                source_content_id="partial-content-3",
                content_key=None,
            )
            _insert_and_commit(
                connection,
                "content_records",
                source_content_id="partial-content-4",
                content_key="partial-content-key-other",
            )

            _insert_and_commit(connection, "tag_demand_contracts")
            _assert_insert_rejected(
                connection,
                "tag_demand_contracts",
                version=2,
                contract_hash="6" * 64,
            )
            _insert_and_commit(
                connection,
                "tag_demand_contracts",
                version=3,
                status="candidate",
                contract_hash="7" * 64,
            )
            _insert_and_commit(
                connection,
                "tag_demand_contracts",
                contract_key="partial-tag-contract-other",
                contract_hash="8" * 64,
            )

            _insert_and_commit(connection, "semantic_tag_facts")
            _assert_insert_rejected(
                connection,
                "semantic_tag_facts",
                fact_version=2,
                payload_hash="9" * 64,
            )
            _insert_and_commit(
                connection,
                "semantic_tag_facts",
                fact_version=3,
                status="candidate",
                payload_hash="a" * 64,
            )
            _insert_and_commit(
                connection,
                "semantic_tag_facts",
                field_key="partial-field-other",
                payload_hash="b" * 64,
            )

            _insert_and_commit(connection, "baseline_truth_revisions")
            _assert_insert_rejected(
                connection,
                "baseline_truth_revisions",
                baseline_set_item_id=9702,
                canonical_hash="c" * 64,
            )
            _insert_and_commit(
                connection,
                "baseline_truth_revisions",
                baseline_set_item_id=9703,
                source_correction_key=None,
                canonical_hash="d" * 64,
            )
            _insert_and_commit(
                connection,
                "baseline_truth_revisions",
                baseline_set_item_id=9704,
                source_correction_key="partial-correction-key-other",
                canonical_hash="f" * 64,
            )
        finally:
            connection.exec_driver_sql("SET SESSION FOREIGN_KEY_CHECKS = 1")
            connection.commit()

                {
                    "asset_version_id": 10301,
                    "field_key": "race-field",
                    "fact_version": 100,
                    "source_evaluation_id": 10311,
                    "source_review_id": 10321,
                    "contract_id": 10331,
                    "payload_hash": "7" * 64,
                },
                {
                    "asset_version_id": 10301,
                    "field_key": "race-field",
                    "fact_version": 101,
                    "source_evaluation_id": 10311,
                    "source_review_id": 10321,
                    "contract_id": 10331,
                    "payload_hash": "8" * 64,
                },
            ),
        ),
        (
            "baseline_truth_revisions",
            (
                {
                    "baseline_set_item_id": 10401,
                    "source_correction_key": "race-correction-key",
                    "canonical_hash": "9" * 64,
                },
                {
                    "baseline_set_item_id": 10402,
                    "source_correction_key": "race-correction-key",
                    "canonical_hash": "a" * 64,
                },
            ),
        ),
    )

    for table_name, competitors in races:
        barrier = threading.Barrier(2)

        def compete(values: dict[str, object]) -> str:
            with mysql_engine.connect() as connection:
                connection.exec_driver_sql(
                    "SET SESSION innodb_lock_wait_timeout = 5"
                )
                connection.exec_driver_sql("SET SESSION FOREIGN_KEY_CHECKS = 0")
                connection.commit()
                transaction = connection.begin()
                try:
                    barrier.wait(timeout=5)
                    _insert_core_row(connection, table_name, **values)
                    transaction.commit()
                    return "committed"
                except IntegrityError:
                    transaction.rollback()
                    return "rejected"
                finally:
                    connection.exec_driver_sql(
                        "SET SESSION FOREIGN_KEY_CHECKS = 1"
                    )
                    connection.commit()

        with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=2) as executor:
            outcomes = list(executor.map(compete, competitors))

        assert Counter(outcomes) == {"committed": 1, "rejected": 1}, table_name


def test_mysql_installed_invariant_inventory_reports_all_database_triggers(
    mysql_engine: Engine,
) -> None:
    with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
        create_mysql_baseline(connection, install_invariants=False)
        install_mysql_invariants(connection)

        assert installed_mysql_invariants(connection) == {
            name
            for name, contract in INVARIANT_CATALOG.items()
            if contract.owner == "database_trigger"
        }


def test_mysql_database_triggers_reject_append_only_mutations_and_deletes(
    mysql_engine: Engine,
) -> None:
    with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
        create_mysql_baseline(connection, install_invariants=False)
        install_mysql_invariants(connection)
        connection.execute(
            Base.metadata.tables["audit_events"].insert(),
            {
                "event_key": "mysql-invariant-audit",
                "category": "runtime",
                "action": "created",
                "subject_type": "run",
                "subject_id": "1",
                "actor": "original",
                "payload_json": "{}",
            },
        )
        connection.exec_driver_sql("SET SESSION FOREIGN_KEY_CHECKS = 0")
        _insert_core_row(
            connection,
            "baseline_truth_revisions",
            baseline_set_item_id=20001,
            source_correction_key="trigger-correction-key",
            canonical_hash="1" * 64,
        )
        connection.exec_driver_sql("SET SESSION FOREIGN_KEY_CHECKS = 1")

    with pytest.raises(DBAPIError, match="immutable"):
        with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
            connection.execute(
                text(
                    "UPDATE audit_events SET actor='changed' "
                    "WHERE event_key='mysql-invariant-audit'"
                )
            )

    with pytest.raises(DBAPIError, match="cannot be deleted"):
        with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
            connection.execute(
                text(
                    "DELETE FROM audit_events "
                    "WHERE event_key='mysql-invariant-audit'"
                )
            )

    with pytest.raises(DBAPIError, match="truth revision is immutable"):
        with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
            connection.execute(
                text(
                    "UPDATE baseline_truth_revisions SET reason='changed' "
                    "WHERE source_correction_key='trigger-correction-key'"
                )
            )

    with pytest.raises(DBAPIError, match="truth revision cannot be deleted"):
        with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
            connection.execute(
                text(
                    "DELETE FROM baseline_truth_revisions "
                    "WHERE source_correction_key='trigger-correction-key'"
                )
            )

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SeededNativeCategory:
    category_key: str
    workflow_version_id: int
    baseline_run_id: int
    production_run_id: int
    prompt_version_ids: tuple[int, ...]
    rule_module_version_ids: tuple[int, ...]
    model_config_id: int
    model_parameters: tuple[tuple[str, int, str], ...]
    expected_result: dict[str, Any]
    active_route: str | None = None


def _frozen_component_selection(
    workflow: WorkflowVersion,
) -> tuple[
    tuple[int, ...],
    tuple[int, ...],
    int,
    tuple[tuple[str, int, str], ...],
]:
    nodes = json.loads(workflow.step_manifest_json)
    prompt_versions: list[int] = []
    rule_versions: list[int] = []
    model_ids: set[int] = set()
    model_parameters: list[tuple[str, int, str]] = []
    for node in nodes:
        config = node.get("config") or {}
        if node.get("kind") == "prompt":
            prompt_versions.append(int(config["prompt_version_id"]))
            model_snapshot = config.get("model_snapshot") or {}
            model_ids.add(int(model_snapshot["source_model_config_id"]))
            model_parameters.append(
                (
                    str(node["node_instance_id"]),
                    int(config["max_tokens"]),
                    str(config["thinking_level"]),
                )
            )
        elif node.get("kind") == "rule_module":
            rule_versions.append(int(config["rule_module_version_id"]))
    assert len(model_ids) == 1
    return (
        tuple(prompt_versions),
        tuple(rule_versions),
        next(iter(model_ids)),
        tuple(model_parameters),
    )


def _ensure_test_categories(db: Session) -> None:
    inspiration = db.scalar(
        select(EvaluationCategoryProfile).where(
            EvaluationCategoryProfile.category_key == "inspiration_image"
        )
    )
    if inspiration is None:
        db.add(
            EvaluationCategoryProfile(
                category_key="inspiration_image",
                display_name="灵感图",
                status="active",
                allowed_mime_types_json='["image/jpeg","image/png"]',
                preprocess_config_json='{"preprocess":"image"}',
                pipeline_config_json="{}",
                created_by="system:mysql-native-smoke",
            )
        )
    seed_defaults(db)


def _existing_category_workflow(
    db: Session,
    category_key: str,
) -> tuple[WorkflowVersion, dict[str, Any], dict[str, Any]]:
    ensure_native_category_workflows(
        db,
        category_key,
        actor="system:mysql-native-smoke",
    )
    revision = db.scalar(
        select(CategoryEvaluationV3Revision)
        .where(
            CategoryEvaluationV3Revision.category_key == category_key,
            CategoryEvaluationV3Revision.status == "active",
        )
        .order_by(
            CategoryEvaluationV3Revision.revision.desc(),
            CategoryEvaluationV3Revision.id.desc(),
        )
    )
    assert revision is not None
    mapping = db.scalar(
        select(LegacyEvaluationRevisionMapping).where(
            LegacyEvaluationRevisionMapping.legacy_revision_id == revision.id
        )
    )
    assert mapping is not None
    workflow = db.get(WorkflowVersion, mapping.workflow_version_id)
    assert workflow is not None
    if category_key == "inspiration_image":
        primary_category, track_key = "建筑设计", "class_one"
    else:
        primary_category, track_key = "家装", "space_building"
    fixture = _fixture(
        revision_bundle(revision),
        primary_category=primary_category,
        track_key=track_key,
    )
    from app.evaluation_v3_pipeline import recompute_qualified_v3
    from app.worker_v3_authoritative import build_v3_authoritative_scoring

    aggregate = recompute_qualified_v3(
        v3_context=fixture["v3_context"],
        precheck=fixture["precheck"],
        dimension_output=fixture["dimension_output"],
    )
    expected = build_v3_authoritative_scoring(
        aggregate,
        precheck=fixture["precheck"],
    )
    asset_snapshot = {
        "workflow_fixture": {
            "schema_version": "native-prompt-fixtures-v1",
            "prompt_outputs": {
                "prompt_a": {"precheck": fixture["precheck"]},
                "prompt_b": {
                    "dimension_output": fixture["dimension_output"]
                },
            },
        }
    }
    return workflow, expected, asset_snapshot

def seed_native_category_fixture(
    engine: Engine,
    category_key: str,
) -> SeededNativeCategory:
    with Session(engine, expire_on_commit=False) as db:
        _ensure_test_categories(db)
        if category_key == "knowledge_graph":
            workflow, expected, asset_snapshot = _knowledge_graph_workflow(db)
            truth_contract_key = "kg.knowledge_graph.domestic.whole.v1"
            expected_level = None
            active_route = "domestic_whole"
        else:
            workflow, expected, asset_snapshot = _existing_category_workflow(
                db,
                category_key,
            )
            truth_contract_key = "level.v1"
            expected_level = str(expected["level"])
            active_route = None

        asset = Asset(
            original_name=f"{category_key}-mysql-native.jpg",
            stored_name=f"{category_key}-mysql-native.jpg",
            mime_type="image/jpeg",
            size_bytes=100,
            sha256={
                "knowledge_graph": "1" * 64,
                "inspiration_image": "2" * 64,
                "model_3d_su": "3" * 64,
            }[category_key],
            category_key=category_key,
        )
        db.add(asset)
        baseline = BaselineSet(
            category_key=category_key,
            name=f"{category_key} MySQL 原生验证",
            description="MySQL 原生工作流确定性夹具",
            default_expected_level=expected_level or "L1",
            fingerprint={
                "knowledge_graph": "4" * 64,
                "inspiration_image": "5" * 64,
                "model_3d_su": "6" * 64,
            }[category_key],
            created_by="mysql-smoke",
        )
        db.add(baseline)
        db.flush()
        asset_snapshot.update(
            {"asset_id": asset.id, "sha256": asset.sha256}
        )
        db.add(
            BaselineSetItem(
                baseline_set_id=baseline.id,
                asset_id=asset.id,
                expected_level=expected_level,
                expected_result_json=canonical_json(
                    expected
                    if category_key == "knowledge_graph"
                    else {"level": expected_level}
                ),
                truth_contract_key=truth_contract_key,
                asset_snapshot_json=canonical_json(asset_snapshot),
            )
        )
        db.flush()
        regression = create_native_baseline_run(
            db,
            baseline_set=baseline,
            workflow_version=workflow,
            actor="mysql-smoke",
            idempotency_key=f"mysql-native-{category_key}",
        )
        production_run_id = regression.items[0].production_run_id
        assert production_run_id is not None
        selection = _frozen_component_selection(workflow)
        seeded = SeededNativeCategory(
            category_key=category_key,
            workflow_version_id=workflow.id,
            baseline_run_id=regression.id,
            production_run_id=production_run_id,
            prompt_version_ids=selection[0],
            rule_module_version_ids=selection[1],
            model_config_id=selection[2],
            model_parameters=selection[3],
            expected_result=expected,
            active_route=active_route,
        )
        db.commit()
        return seeded


def execute_fixture_native_run(
    engine: Engine,
    seeded: SeededNativeCategory,
) -> int:
    import app.workflow_fixture_executor as executor

    sessions = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    original_session_local = executor.SessionLocal
    executor.SessionLocal = sessions
    processed = 0
    try:
        while process_runtime_step_once(
            f"mysql-native-{seeded.category_key}-worker"
        ):
            processed += 1
            if processed > 40:
                raise AssertionError("原生工作流未在 40 个步骤内结束")
    finally:
        executor.SessionLocal = original_session_local
    assert processed > 0
    return seeded.production_run_id


def load_native_run_evidence(
    engine: Engine,
    run_id: int,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    with Session(engine) as db:
        run = db.get(ProductionRun, run_id)
        assert run is not None
        snapshot = json.loads(run.snapshot_json)
        nodes = snapshot["workflow"]["manifest"]["nodes"]
        prompts = [node for node in nodes if node["kind"] == "prompt"]
        rules = [node for node in nodes if node["kind"] == "rule_module"]
        model_ids = {
            int(node["config"]["model_snapshot"]["source_model_config_id"])
            for node in prompts
        }
        attempts = {
            item.step_key: {
                "status": item.status,
                "error_code": item.last_error_code,
                "output": json.loads(item.output_manifest_json),
            }
            for item in db.scalars(
                select(ProductionStepAttempt).where(
                    ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == run_id
                )
            ).all()
        }
        result = db.scalar(
            select(EvaluationResult).where(
                EvaluationResult.production_run_id == run_id
            )
        )
        baseline = db.get(
            BaselineRegressionRun,
            snapshot["runtime_context"]["baseline_run_id"],
        )
        return {
            "status": run.status,
            "category_key": run.category_key,
            "workflow_version_id": snapshot["workflow"][
                "workflow_version_id"
            ],
            "prompt_version_ids": tuple(
                int(node["config"]["prompt_version_id"])
                for node in prompts
            ),
            "rule_module_version_ids": tuple(
                int(node["config"]["rule_module_version_id"])
                for node in rules
            ),
            "model_config_id": next(iter(model_ids)),
            "model_parameters": tuple(
                (
                    node["node_instance_id"],
                    int(node["config"]["max_tokens"]),
                    str(node["config"]["thinking_level"]),
                )
                for node in prompts
            ),
            "attempts": attempts,
            "normalized_result": (
                json.loads(result.normalized_result_json)
                if result is not None
                else None
            ),
            "baseline_status": baseline.status if baseline is not None else None,
        }
