@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "category_key",
    ["knowledge_graph", "inspiration_image", "model_3d_su"],
)
def test_native_workflow_freezes_and_executes_selected_component_versions(
    mysql_engine: Engine,
    category_key: str,
) -> None:
    with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
        create_mysql_baseline(connection)
    seeded = seed_native_category_fixture(mysql_engine, category_key)

    run_id = execute_fixture_native_run(mysql_engine, seeded)
    evidence = load_native_run_evidence(mysql_engine, run_id)

    assert evidence["status"] == "succeeded"
    assert evidence["baseline_status"] == "completed"
    assert evidence["workflow_version_id"] == seeded.workflow_version_id
    assert evidence["prompt_version_ids"] == seeded.prompt_version_ids
    assert evidence["rule_module_version_ids"] == (
        seeded.rule_module_version_ids
    )
    assert evidence["model_config_id"] == seeded.model_config_id
    assert evidence["model_parameters"] == seeded.model_parameters

    attempts = evidence["attempts"]
    if category_key == "knowledge_graph":
        assert seeded.active_route is not None
        assert attempts[f"prompt_{seeded.active_route}"]["status"] == (
            "succeeded"
        )
        assert attempts[f"project_{seeded.active_route}"]["status"] == (
            "succeeded"
        )
        for route in (
            "domestic_whole",
            "domestic_single",
            "overseas_whole",
            "overseas_single",
        ):
            if route == seeded.active_route:
                continue
            assert attempts[f"prompt_{route}"]["status"] == "skipped"
            assert attempts[f"project_{route}"]["status"] == "skipped"
            assert attempts[f"prompt_{route}"]["error_code"] == (
                "route_not_selected"
            )
            assert attempts[f"project_{route}"]["error_code"] == (
                "route_not_selected"
            )
        assert evidence["normalized_result"] == seeded.expected_result
    else:
        assert attempts["prompt_a"]["status"] == "succeeded"
        assert attempts["prompt_b"]["status"] == "succeeded"
        assert len(seeded.rule_module_version_ids) == 7
        for node_id in (
            "eligibility_redline_1",
            "track_routing_2",
            "media_adjustment_3",
            "quality_cap_exception_4",
            "dimension_scoring_5",
            "anchor_calibration_6",
            "score_level_matcher_7",
        ):
            assert attempts[node_id]["status"] == "succeeded"
        assert evidence["normalized_result"]["level"] == (
            seeded.expected_result["level"]
        )


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "category_key",
    ["knowledge_graph", "inspiration_image", "model_3d_su"],
)
def test_mysql_native_correction_analysis_recommendation_rerun_loop(
    mysql_engine: Engine,
    category_key: str,
) -> None:
    from app.baseline_correction_orchestration import refresh_correction_run
    from app.workflow_correction import (
        analyze_workflow_corrections,
        append_node_correction_and_replay,
        compose_candidate_workflow,
        recommend_component_versions,
        start_single_candidate_regression,
    )

    with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
        create_mysql_baseline(connection)
    seeded = seed_native_category_fixture(mysql_engine, category_key)
    execute_fixture_native_run(mysql_engine, seeded)

    with Session(mysql_engine, expire_on_commit=False) as db:
        source = db.get(BaselineRegressionRun, seeded.baseline_run_id)
        assert source is not None and source.status == "completed"
        source_item = source.items[0]
        if category_key == "knowledge_graph":
            corrected_node = "prompt_domestic_whole"
            corrected_field = "style"
            corrected_value: Any = "现代简约,侘寂风"
        else:
            corrected_node = "prompt_b"
            corrected_field = "dimension_output.aesthetic_score"
            corrected_value = 55
        replay = append_node_correction_and_replay(
            db,
            run_id=seeded.production_run_id,
            node_instance_id=corrected_node,
            field_path=corrected_field,
            value=corrected_value,
            reason="人工核验后修正节点结果",
            actor="运营验收",
            correction_key=f"mysql-{category_key}-correction",
            evidence=[{"text": "MySQL 原生闭环人工证据"}],
        )
        assert corrected_node in replay.affected_nodes
        if category_key == "knowledge_graph":
            assert replay.truth_revision == 1
            assert replay.effective_next_run is True

        correction = BaselineCorrectionRun(
            idempotency_key=f"mysql-{category_key}-candidate-loop",
            baseline_run_id=source.id,
            category_key=category_key,
            selected_item_ids_json=canonical_json([source_item.id]),
            input_snapshot_json="{}",
            created_by="mysql-smoke",
        )
        db.add(correction)
        db.flush()
        analysis = analyze_workflow_corrections(
            db,
            source.id,
            [source_item.id],
        )
        assert analysis.blockers == ()
        recommendations = recommend_component_versions(db, analysis)
        assert recommendations
        assert {item.node_instance_id for item in recommendations} == {
            corrected_node
        }
        candidate = compose_candidate_workflow(
            db,
            seeded.workflow_version_id,
            recommendations,
            correction.id,
            "mysql-smoke",
        )
        assert candidate.id != seeded.workflow_version_id
        candidate_run = start_single_candidate_regression(
            db,
            correction,
            "mysql-smoke",
        )
        assert len(candidate_run.items) == 1
        candidate_production_id = candidate_run.items[0].production_run_id
        assert candidate_production_id is not None
        correction_id = correction.id
        candidate_run_id = candidate_run.id
        candidate_workflow_id = candidate.id
        db.commit()

    execute_fixture_native_run(
        mysql_engine,
        replace(
            seeded,
            workflow_version_id=candidate_workflow_id,
            baseline_run_id=candidate_run_id,
            production_run_id=candidate_production_id,
        ),
    )

    with Session(mysql_engine) as db:
        correction = db.get(BaselineCorrectionRun, correction_id)
        candidate_run = db.get(BaselineRegressionRun, candidate_run_id)
        assert correction is not None and candidate_run is not None
        assert candidate_run.status == "completed"
        refresh_correction_run(db, correction)
        assert correction.status == "awaiting_decision"
        assert correction.stage == "decision"
        report = json.loads(correction.report_json)["candidate_regression"]
        assert report["run_id"] == candidate_run.id
        assert isinstance(report["approval_allowed"], bool)

from __future__ import annotations

import importlib.util

def _integration_script() -> Any:
    script = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / "scripts" / "mysql-integration.py"
    specification = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(
        "labelsystem_mysql_reconciliation_script",
        script,
    )
    assert specification is not None
    assert specification.loader is not None
    module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(specification)
    specification.loader.exec_module(module)
    return module


def _workflow_source(
    path: Path,
    *,
    legacy_v81: bool = False,
    legacy_v82: bool = False,
    legacy_v89: bool = False,
) -> Path:
    assert sum((legacy_v81, legacy_v82, legacy_v89)) <= 1
    source = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{path}")
    now = datetime(2026, 8, 28, 6, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    try:
        Base.metadata.create_all(source)
        with source.begin() as connection:
            connection.execute(
                Base.metadata.tables["workflow_definitions"].insert(),
                {
                    "id": 1,
                    "workflow_key": "mysql-reconcile-fixture",
                    "name": "MySQL 对账夹具",
                    "description": "",
                    "owner": "test",
                    "allowed_categories_json": '["knowledge_graph"]',
                    "status": "active",
                    "created_by": "test",
                    "created_at": now,
                    "updated_at": now,
                },
            )
            connection.execute(
                Base.metadata.tables["workflow_versions"].insert(),
                {
                    "id": 1,
                    "workflow_definition_id": 1,
                    "version": "native-v1",
                    "status": "active",
                    "workflow_schema_version": "workflow-v3",
                    "step_manifest_json": '[{"b":2,"a":1}]',
                    "edge_manifest_json": "[]",
                    "input_schema_json": "{}",
                    "output_schema_json": "{}",
                    "resource_policy_json": "{}",
                    "canonical_hash": "0" * 64,
                    "validation_report_json": "{}",
                    "created_by": "test",
                    "created_at": now,
                    "updated_at": now,
                },
            )
            if not legacy_v81:
                connection.execute(
                    Base.metadata.tables["workflow_snapshot_blobs"].insert(),
                    {
                        "id": 1,
                        "snapshot_hash": "1" * 64,
                        "canonical_size_bytes": 2,
                        "compressed_size_bytes": 2,
                        "compression": "gzip",
                        "payload_bytes": b"\x00\xff",
                        "created_at": now,
                    },
                )
            if legacy_v81 or legacy_v82 or legacy_v89:
                connection.exec_driver_sql(
                    "CREATE TABLE schema_migrations ("
                    "version INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, name TEXT NOT NULL)"
                )
                connection.exec_driver_sql(
                    "INSERT INTO schema_migrations(version, name) "
                    "VALUES (?, ?)",
                    (
                        81 if legacy_v81 else 82 if legacy_v82 else 89,
                        (
                            "add_baseline_truth_revisions"
                            if legacy_v81
                            else (
                                "add_runtime_foundation"
                                if legacy_v82
                                else "scope_nas_source_uri_to_category"
                            )
                        ),
                    ),
                )
                connection.exec_driver_sql(
                    "DROP TABLE workflow_model_daily_budgets"
                )
                for table_name in (
                    "paid_acceptance_call_reservations",
                    "paid_acceptance_campaigns",
                    "workflow_external_waits",
                    "workflow_model_media_usage",
                    "human_review_overflow_facts",
                    "human_review_daily_batch_items",
                    "human_review_daily_batches",
                    "human_review_quota_policies",
                ):
                    if not legacy_v89:
                        connection.exec_driver_sql(f"DROP TABLE {table_name}")
                if legacy_v89:
                    connection.exec_driver_sql("DROP TABLE image_first_seen_cache")
                else:
                    if legacy_v81:
                        connection.exec_driver_sql("DROP TABLE runtime_outbox_events")
                        connection.exec_driver_sql("DROP TABLE workflow_snapshot_blobs")
                    else:
                        connection.exec_driver_sql("DROP TABLE runtime_outbox_events")
                        connection.exec_driver_sql(
                            """
                        CREATE TABLE runtime_outbox_events (
                            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                            event_key VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
                            target_contract_key VARCHAR(160) NOT NULL,
                            business_idempotency_key VARCHAR(240) NOT NULL,
                            mechanism_version_id INTEGER NOT NULL
                                REFERENCES workflow_versions(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
                            payload_blob_id INTEGER NOT NULL
                                REFERENCES workflow_snapshot_blobs(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
                            payload_hash VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL,
                            status VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'queued',
                            available_at DATETIME NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                            attempt_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
                            max_attempts INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 5,
                            lease_owner VARCHAR(120),
                            lease_token VARCHAR(80),
                            leased_at DATETIME,
                            heartbeat_at DATETIME,
                            lease_expires_at DATETIME,
                            last_error_code VARCHAR(80) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
                            last_error_message TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
                            delivery_receipt_json TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}',
                            delivered_at DATETIME,
                            dead_lettered_at DATETIME,
                            created_at DATETIME NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                            updated_at DATETIME NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                            UNIQUE(target_contract_key, business_idempotency_key)
                        )
                            """
                        )
                    connection.exec_driver_sql("DROP TABLE upstream_intake_events")
                    connection.exec_driver_sql("DROP TABLE connector_pause_states")
                    connection.exec_driver_sql("DROP TABLE connector_contract_versions")
    finally:
        source.dispose()
    path.chmod(0o400)
    return path


def _create_and_import(
    source: Path,
    target: Engine,
    checkpoint_dir: Path,
) -> None:
    with target.begin() as connection:
        create_mysql_baseline(connection, install_invariants=False)
    import_sqlite_snapshot_to_mysql(
        source,
        target,
        checkpoint_dir,
        batch_size=20,
    )


def test_binary_values_have_a_deterministic_canonical_form() -> None:
    column = Column("payload_bytes", LargeBinary)

    assert canonical_database_value(b"\x00\xff", column) == b"Y\x00\xff"
    assert canonical_database_value(memoryview(b"\x00\xff"), column) == (
        b"Y\x00\xff"
    )


def test_fractional_value_in_integer_column_is_rejected() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ReconciliationError, match="非法整数"):
        canonical_database_value(1.5, Column("item_id", Integer))


def test_float_value_in_boolean_column_is_rejected() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ReconciliationError, match="非法布尔值"):
        canonical_database_value(1.0, Column("enabled", Boolean))


def test_auto_increment_reconciliation_bypasses_cached_mysql_statistics() -> None:
    class CachedStatisticsConnection:
        def __init__(self) -> None:
            self.fresh = False

        def exec_driver_sql(self, statement: str) -> None:
            if statement == "SET SESSION information_schema_stats_expiry = 0":
                self.fresh = True

        def execute(self, _statement: object) -> list[SimpleNamespace]:
            return [
                SimpleNamespace(
                    TABLE_NAME=table.name,
                    AUTO_INCREMENT=(
                        11 if self.fresh and table.name == "users" else 1
                    ),
                )
                for table in Base.metadata.tables.values()
                if table._autoincrement_column is not None
            ]

    connection = CachedStatisticsConnection()
    tables = {
        table.name: SimpleNamespace(
            target_primary_key_max=10 if table.name == "users" else None
        )
        for table in Base.metadata.tables.values()
    }

    states = _auto_increment_states(connection, tables)  # type: ignore[arg-type]

    assert states["users"].next_value == 11
    assert states["users"].safe is True


def test_mysql_reconciliation_accepts_every_mapped_fact_and_structure(
    mysql_engine: Engine,
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    source = _workflow_source(tmp_path / "source.db")
    _create_and_import(source, mysql_engine, tmp_path / "checkpoints")

    report = reconcile_mysql_databases(source, mysql_engine)
    assert_mysql_critical_invariants(report)

    assert set(report.tables) == set(Base.metadata.tables)
    assert len(report.tables) == 121
    assert all(item.matches for item in report.tables.values())
    assert report.target_mysql_version.startswith("8.4.")
    assert report.target_schema_version == max(
        migration.version for migration in MIGRATIONS
    )
    assert len(report.installed_invariant_triggers) == 48
    assert len(report.generated_unique_guards) == 8
    assert len(report.generated_guard_indexes) == 8
    assert report.source_uri_hash_contract is True
    assert all(fact["compatible"] for fact in report.schema_facts.values())
    assert all(state.safe for state in report.auto_increments.values())
    assert len(report.data_evidence_sha256) == 64
    rendered_json = json.dumps(
        report.as_dict(),
        ensure_ascii=False,
        sort_keys=True,
    )
    rendered_markdown = report.to_markdown(accepted=True)
    assert "SQLite → MySQL 8.4 对账报告" in rendered_markdown
    assert "mysql+pymysql://" not in rendered_json + rendered_markdown
    assert "must-not-echo" not in rendered_json + rendered_markdown
    missing_guard = replace(
        report,
        generated_unique_guards=report.generated_unique_guards[:-1],
    )
    with pytest.raises(ReconciliationError, match="generated_guards"):
        assert_mysql_critical_invariants(missing_guard)


def test_mysql_reconciliation_accepts_additive_tables_missing_from_v81_snapshot(
    mysql_engine: Engine,
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    source = _workflow_source(tmp_path / "source-v81.db", legacy_v81=True)
    _create_and_import(source, mysql_engine, tmp_path / "checkpoints-v81")

    report = reconcile_mysql_databases(source, mysql_engine)
    assert_mysql_critical_invariants(report)

    assert report.source_schema_version == 81
    assert report.target_schema_version == max(
        migration.version for migration in MIGRATIONS
    )
    assert report.forward_compatible_source is True
    for table_name in (
        "workflow_snapshot_blobs",
        "runtime_outbox_events",
        "connector_contract_versions",
        "upstream_intake_events",
        "connector_pause_states",
        "human_review_quota_policies",
        "human_review_daily_batches",
        "human_review_daily_batch_items",
        "human_review_overflow_facts",
        "workflow_model_media_usage",
        "workflow_external_waits",
        "paid_acceptance_campaigns",
        "paid_acceptance_call_reservations",
        "workflow_model_daily_budgets",
    ):
        assert report.schema_facts[table_name]["source_absent"] is True
        assert report.schema_facts[table_name]["compatible"] is True
        assert report.tables[table_name].source_row_count == 0
        assert report.tables[table_name].target_row_count == 0
        assert report.tables[table_name].matches is True

def test_mysql_reconciliation_blocks_a_changed_workflow_fact(
    mysql_engine: Engine,
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    source = _workflow_source(tmp_path / "source.db")
    _create_and_import(source, mysql_engine, tmp_path / "checkpoints")
    with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
        connection.execute(text("DELETE FROM workflow_versions WHERE id=1"))

    report = reconcile_mysql_databases(source, mysql_engine)

    with pytest.raises(ReconciliationError, match="workflow_versions"):
        assert_mysql_critical_invariants(report)


def test_mysql_reconciliation_cli_writes_private_safe_receipts(
    tmp_path: Path,
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    module = _integration_script()
    source = tmp_path / "frozen.db"
    source.write_bytes(b"sqlite-fixture")
    accounts = tmp_path / "accounts.json"
    accounts.write_text("{}", encoding="utf-8")
    json_report = tmp_path / "reports" / "reconciliation.json"
    markdown_report = tmp_path / "reports" / "reconciliation.md"
    calls: dict[str, object] = {}

    class FakeConnection:
        def __enter__(self) -> "FakeConnection":
            return self

        def __exit__(self, *_args: object) -> None:
            return None

    class FakeEngine:
        def connect(self) -> FakeConnection:
            return FakeConnection()

        def dispose(self) -> None:
            calls["disposed"] = True

    fake_engine = FakeEngine()
    fake_report = SimpleNamespace(
        as_dict=lambda: {
            "source": str(source.resolve()),
            "target_schema": "labels_migration",
            "tables": {"users": {"matches": True}},
        },
        to_markdown=lambda *, accepted, error="": (
            "# SQLite → MySQL 8.4 对账报告\n\n"
            f"- 结论：{'通过' if accepted else '阻断'}\n"
            f"{error}\n"
        ),
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        module,
        "mysql_database_url_from_references",
        lambda accounts_file, *, role, database: calls.update(
            {
                "accounts": accounts_file,
                "role": role,
                "database": database,
            }
        )
        or "mysql+pymysql://migration:must-not-echo@localhost/labels",
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        module,
        "create_mysql_migration_engine",
        lambda url: calls.setdefault("url", url) and fake_engine,
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        module,
        "assert_mysql_runtime_contract",
        lambda connection: calls.setdefault("contract", connection),
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        module,
        "reconcile_mysql_databases",
        lambda source_path, target: calls.update(
            {"source": source_path, "target": target}
        )
        or fake_report,
        raising=False,
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        module,
        "assert_mysql_critical_invariants",
        lambda report: calls.setdefault("asserted", report),
        raising=False,
    )
    output = StringIO()

    exit_code = module.main(
        [
            "reconcile-snapshot",
            "--source",
            str(source),
            "--database",
            "labels_migration",
            "--accounts",
            str(accounts),
            "--json-report",
            str(json_report),
            "--markdown-report",
            str(markdown_report),
        ],
        output=output,
    )

    payload = json.loads(output.getvalue())
    receipt = json.loads(json_report.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    assert exit_code == 0
    assert calls["role"] == "migration"
    assert calls["database"] == "labels_migration"
    assert calls["source"] == source
    assert calls["target"] is fake_engine
    assert calls["asserted"] is fake_report
    assert calls["disposed"] is True
    assert receipt["accepted"] is True
    assert receipt["error"] == ""
    assert receipt["report"]["target_schema"] == "labels_migration"
    assert "结论：通过" in markdown_report.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    assert stat.S_IMODE(json_report.stat().st_mode) == 0o600
    assert stat.S_IMODE(markdown_report.stat().st_mode) == 0o600
    assert payload == {
        "accepted": True,
        "command": "reconcile-snapshot",
        "json_report": str(json_report.resolve()),
        "markdown_report": str(markdown_report.resolve()),
        "status": "ok",
        "target_schema": "labels_migration",
    }
    rendered = output.getvalue() + json_report.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    rendered += markdown_report.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    assert "must-not-echo" not in rendered

    def block_reconciliation(_report: object) -> None:
        raise ReconciliationError("tables=workflow_versions")

    blocked_json = tmp_path / "reports" / "blocked.json"
    blocked_markdown = tmp_path / "reports" / "blocked.md"
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        module,
        "assert_mysql_critical_invariants",
        block_reconciliation,
    )
    blocked_output = StringIO()

    blocked_exit_code = module.main(
        [
            "reconcile-snapshot",
            "--source",
            str(source),
            "--database",
            "labels_migration",
            "--accounts",
            str(accounts),
            "--json-report",
            str(blocked_json),
            "--markdown-report",
            str(blocked_markdown),
        ],
        output=blocked_output,
    )

    blocked_receipt = json.loads(blocked_json.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    assert blocked_exit_code == 2
    assert json.loads(blocked_output.getvalue())["status"] == "blocked"
    assert blocked_receipt["accepted"] is False
    assert blocked_receipt["error"] == "tables=workflow_versions"
    assert "结论：阻断" in blocked_markdown.read_text(encoding="utf-8")

from __future__ import annotations

import re

from sqlalchemy import (
    CheckConstraint,
    DateTime,
    Text,
    UniqueConstraint,
    inspect,
    text,
)
from sqlalchemy.dialects import mysql
from sqlalchemy.engine import Engine
from sqlalchemy.exc import DBAPIError, IntegrityError
from sqlalchemy.schema import CreateTable

from app import models  # noqa: F401
from app.database import Base
from app.migrations.mysql_baseline import (
    EXPECTED_PARTIAL_INDEX_NAMES,
    MySQLBaselineError,
    assert_mysql_schema_version,
    create_mysql_baseline,
    mysql_metadata,
    mysql_schema_fingerprint,
    upgrade_mysql_schema,
)
from app.migrations.runner import MIGRATIONS


LATEST_SCHEMA_VERSION = max(migration.version for migration in MIGRATIONS)
MEDIA_EVIDENCE_COLUMNS = {
    "fidelity_mode",
    "fidelity_value",
    "preprocess_mode",
    "preprocess_value",
    "policy_hash",
    "capability_profile_key",
    "normalized_pixel_hash",
    "compression_ms",
    "saved_bytes",
    "saved_ratio_ppm",
    "provider_max_bytes",
    "operational_max_bytes",
    "size_gate_result",
    "estimation_formula_version",
}


def _compiled_tables() -> str:
    metadata = mysql_metadata()
    return "\n".join(
        str(CreateTable(table).compile(dialect=mysql.dialect()))
        for table in metadata.tables.values()
    ).lower()


def _check_sql(table_name: str, constraint_name: str) -> str:
    table = mysql_metadata().tables[table_name]
    for constraint in table.constraints:
        if isinstance(constraint, CheckConstraint) and constraint.name == constraint_name:
            return str(constraint.sqltext).lower()
    raise AssertionError(f"缺少约束 {table_name}.{constraint_name}")


def test_mysql_metadata_compiles_every_table_without_sqlite_fragments() -> None:
    metadata = mysql_metadata()
    ddl = _compiled_tables()

    assert len(metadata.tables) == 153
    assert {
        "connector_contract_versions",
        "upstream_intake_events",
        "connector_pause_states",
        "model_quota_pools",
        "model_quota_leases",
        "model_quota_minute_windows",
        "image_first_seen_cache",
        "workflow_foreach_items",
    } <= set(metadata.tables)
    assert " glob " not in ddl
    assert "json_type(definition_json, '$')" not in ddl
    assert "json_extract(expected_result_json, '$.level') = expected_level" not in ddl
    assert (
        "json_unquote(json_extract(expected_result_json, '$.level')) = expected_level"
        in ddl
    )
    assert "regexp_like(" in ddl


def test_mysql_metadata_carries_foreach_and_keyed_invocation_identity() -> None:
    metadata = mysql_metadata()
    foreach = metadata.tables["workflow_foreach_items"]
    invocations = metadata.tables["workflow_model_invocations"]

    assert {
        "production_run_id",
        "parent_step_attempt_id",
        "item_key",
        "input_hash",
        "output_hash",
    } <= set(foreach.c.keys())
    assert "invocation_key" in invocations.c
    unique_columns = {
        tuple(column.name for column in constraint.columns)
        for constraint in invocations.constraints
        if isinstance(constraint, UniqueConstraint)
    }
    assert ("step_attempt_id", "invocation_key") in unique_columns
    assert ("step_attempt_id",) not in unique_columns


def test_mysql_metadata_translates_json_root_kinds_and_scalar_strings() -> None:
    object_check = _check_sql(
        "runtime_audit_events",
        "ck_runtime_audit_events_details_json",
    )
    array_check = _check_sql(
        "evaluation_production_runs",
        "ck_evaluation_production_runs_blockers_json",
    )
    level_check = _check_sql(
        "baseline_truth_revisions",
        "ck_baseline_truth_revisions_shape",
    )

    assert "lower(json_type(json_extract(details_json, '$'))) = 'object'" in object_check
    assert "lower(json_type(json_extract(blockers_json, '$'))) = 'array'" in array_check
    assert (
        "lower(json_type(json_extract(expected_result_json, '$.level'))) = 'string'"
        in level_check
    )
    assert (
        "json_unquote(json_extract(expected_result_json, '$.level')) = expected_level"
        in level_check
    )


def test_mysql_metadata_carries_vision_profile_and_media_evidence() -> None:
    metadata = mysql_metadata()
    assert "vision_capability_profile_json" in metadata.tables["model_configs"].c
    assert "vision_capability_profile_json" in metadata.tables[
        "model_registry_entries"
    ].c
    assert "pricing_contract_json" in metadata.tables["model_configs"].c
    assert "pricing_contract_json" in metadata.tables[
        "model_registry_entries"
    ].c
    assert {
        "pricing_snapshot_json",
        "pricing_tier_key",
        "actual_cost_micros",
    } <= set(metadata.tables["workflow_model_invocations"].c.keys())
    assert MEDIA_EVIDENCE_COLUMNS <= {
        column.name
        for column in metadata.tables["workflow_model_media_usage"].c
    }
    assert (
        "lower(json_type(json_extract(vision_capability_profile_json, '$'))) "
        "= 'object'"
        in _check_sql(
            "model_configs",
            "ck_model_configs_vision_capability_profile",
        )
    )
    assert (
        "lower(json_type(json_extract(pricing_contract_json, '$'))) "
        "= 'object'"
        in _check_sql(
            "model_configs",
            "ck_model_configs_pricing_contract",
        )
    )


def test_mysql_metadata_translates_hex_contract_to_case_sensitive_regexp() -> None:
    check = _check_sql(
        "evaluation_production_runs",

    )

    assert "glob" not in check
    assert "regexp_like(request_hash, '^[0-9a-f]+$', 'c')" in check


def test_mysql_metadata_quotes_reserved_column_names_inside_checks() -> None:
    cursor_check = _check_sql(
        "consumer_sync_checkpoints",
        "ck_consumer_sync_checkpoints_cursor",
    )
    role_check = _check_sql(
        "model_registry_entries",
        "ck_model_registry_role",
    )

    assert cursor_check == "`cursor` >= 0"
    assert role_check == "`role` in ('main','tuning','benchmark')"


def test_mysql_metadata_removes_all_nine_unsupported_partial_indexes() -> None:
    metadata = mysql_metadata()
    names = {
        index.name
        for table in metadata.tables.values()
        for index in table.indexes
    }
    source_names = {
        index.name
        for table in Base.metadata.tables.values()
        for index in table.indexes
        if index.dialect_options["sqlite"].get("where") is not None
    }

    assert len(EXPECTED_PARTIAL_INDEX_NAMES) == 9
    assert not names.intersection(EXPECTED_PARTIAL_INDEX_NAMES)
    assert source_names == EXPECTED_PARTIAL_INDEX_NAMES


def test_mysql_metadata_defers_auto_increment_checks_to_triggers() -> None:
    trigger_backed = {
        "ck_dimension_schemas_parent_not_self",
        "ck_dimension_schemas_core_not_self",
    }
    native_singletons = {
        "ck_queue_scheduler_state_singleton",
        "ck_automation_policy_singleton",
    }
    mysql_checks = {
        constraint.name
        for table in mysql_metadata().tables.values()
        for constraint in table.constraints
        if isinstance(constraint, CheckConstraint)
    }
    canonical_checks = {
        constraint.name
        for table in Base.metadata.tables.values()
        for constraint in table.constraints
        if isinstance(constraint, CheckConstraint)
    }

    assert trigger_backed | native_singletons <= canonical_checks
    assert not trigger_backed.intersection(mysql_checks)
    assert native_singletons <= mysql_checks


def test_mysql_metadata_preserves_microseconds_and_case_sensitive_text() -> None:
    expected_datetime_columns = sum(
        isinstance(column.type, DateTime)
        for table in Base.metadata.tables.values()
        for column in table.columns
    )
    metadata = mysql_metadata()
    datetime_columns = [
        column
        for table in metadata.tables.values()
        for column in table.columns
        if isinstance(column.type, mysql.DATETIME)
    ]

    assert len(datetime_columns) == expected_datetime_columns
    assert all(column.type.fsp == 6 for column in datetime_columns)
    for table_name, column_name in (
        ("workflow_definitions", "workflow_key"),
        ("runtime_outbox_events", "event_key"),
        ("content_records", "content_key"),
    ):
        assert (
            metadata.tables[table_name].c[column_name].type.collation
            == "utf8mb4_0900_as_cs"
        )


def test_mysql_metadata_preserves_unbounded_text_capacity() -> None:
    metadata = mysql_metadata()
    source_text_columns = [
        (table.name, column.name)
        for table in Base.metadata.tables.values()
        for column in table.columns
        if isinstance(column.type, Text)
    ]
    mysql_longtext_columns = [
        (table.name, column.name)
        for table in metadata.tables.values()
        for column in table.columns
        if isinstance(column.type, mysql.LONGTEXT)
    ]

    assert len(source_text_columns) == 324
    assert mysql_longtext_columns == source_text_columns
    assert isinstance(
        metadata.tables["baseline_regression_runs"]
        .c.correction_contract_json.type,
        mysql.LONGTEXT,
    )


def test_mysql_metadata_matches_current_timestamp_to_datetime_precision() -> None:
    metadata = mysql_metadata()

    for table_name in ("dimension_schemas", "dimension_route_policies"):
        created_at = metadata.tables[table_name].c.created_at
        ddl = str(
            CreateTable(metadata.tables[table_name]).compile(
                dialect=mysql.dialect()
            )
        ).lower()

        assert isinstance(created_at.type, mysql.DATETIME)
        assert created_at.type.fsp == 6
        assert str(created_at.server_default.arg).lower() == "current_timestamp(6)"
        assert "created_at datetime(6) not null default current_timestamp(6)" in ddl


def test_mysql_metadata_does_not_mutate_canonical_datetime_types() -> None:
    expected_datetime_columns = {
        (table.name, column.name)
        for table in Base.metadata.tables.values()
        for column in table.columns
        if isinstance(column.type, DateTime)
    }
    mysql_metadata()

    canonical_datetime_columns = {
        (table.name, column.name): column
        for table in Base.metadata.tables.values()
        for column in table.columns
        if isinstance(column.type, DateTime)
    }

    assert set(canonical_datetime_columns) == expected_datetime_columns
    assert all(
        not isinstance(column.type, mysql.DATETIME)
        for column in canonical_datetime_columns.values()
    )


def test_mysql_metadata_wraps_every_text_default_as_an_expression() -> None:
    metadata = mysql_metadata()
    text_defaults = [
        str(column.server_default.arg)
        for table in metadata.tables.values()
        for column in table.columns
        if isinstance(column.type, mysql.LONGTEXT)
        and column.server_default is not None
    ]
    users_ddl = str(
        CreateTable(metadata.tables["users"]).compile(dialect=mysql.dialect())
    ).lower()

    assert len(text_defaults) == 84
    assert all(default.startswith("(") and default.endswith(")") for default in text_defaults)
    assert "permissions_json longtext" in users_ddl
    assert "default ('[]')" in users_ddl


def test_mysql_metadata_preserves_category_scoped_long_source_uri_digest() -> None:
    assets = mysql_metadata().tables["assets"]
    source_unique_constraints = [
        constraint
        for constraint in assets.constraints
        if isinstance(constraint, UniqueConstraint)
        and [column.name for column in constraint.columns] == ["source_uri"]
    ]
    digest_unique_constraints = [
        constraint
        for constraint in assets.constraints
        if isinstance(constraint, UniqueConstraint)
        and constraint.name == "uq_assets_source_uri_sha256_category"
    ]

    assert assets.c.source_uri.type.length == 1000
    assert assets.c.source_uri.type.collation == "utf8mb4_0900_bin"
    assert source_unique_constraints == []
    assert len(digest_unique_constraints) == 1
    assert [
        column.name for column in digest_unique_constraints[0].columns
    ] == ["source_uri_sha256", "category_key"]
    assert str(assets.c.source_uri_sha256.type).lower() == "binary(32)"
    assert assets.c.source_uri_sha256.computed is not None
    assert (
        str(assets.c.source_uri_sha256.computed.sqltext).lower()
        == "unhex(sha2(source_uri, 256))"
    )


def test_mysql_baseline_creates_every_table_and_records_latest_migration(
    mysql_engine: Engine,
) -> None:
    with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
        create_mysql_baseline(connection, install_invariants=False)

    actual = set(inspect(mysql_engine).get_table_names())
    with mysql_engine.connect() as connection:
        recorded = connection.execute(
            text("SELECT version, name FROM schema_migrations ORDER BY version")
        ).all()

    assert set(Base.metadata.tables) <= actual
    assert "schema_migrations" in actual
    assert recorded == [(migration.version, migration.name) for migration in MIGRATIONS]
    assert recorded[-1][0] == LATEST_SCHEMA_VERSION


def test_mysql_baseline_refuses_a_nonempty_target_schema(
    mysql_engine: Engine,
) -> None:
    with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
        connection.exec_driver_sql("CREATE TABLE existing_fact (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY)")

    with pytest.raises(MySQLBaselineError, match="空 schema"):
        with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
            create_mysql_baseline(connection, install_invariants=False)

    assert "existing_fact" in inspect(mysql_engine).get_table_names()

def test_mysql_baseline_rejects_dimension_schema_self_reference_on_insert(
    mysql_engine: Engine,
) -> None:
    with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
        create_mysql_baseline(connection, install_invariants=False)

    with pytest.raises(DBAPIError, match="dimension schema cannot reference itself"):
        with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
            connection.execute(
                text(
                    "INSERT INTO dimension_schemas "
                    "(id, schema_key, version, schema_type, family_key, "
                    "display_name, status, parent_schema_id, definition_json, "
                    "canonical_hash, created_by) VALUES "
                    "(9001, 'self-parent', 'v1', 'extension', 'common', "
                    "'Self parent', 'draft', 9001, '{}', :canonical_hash, 'test')"
                ),
                {"canonical_hash": "a" * 64},
            )

def test_mysql_baseline_enforces_category_scoped_uniqueness_for_long_source_uri(
    mysql_engine: Engine,
) -> None:
    with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
        create_mysql_baseline(connection, install_invariants=False)

    source_uri = "https://example.test/assets/" + "界" * 850
    insert = text(
        "INSERT INTO assets "
        "(original_name, stored_name, source_uri, mime_type, size_bytes, sha256, "
        "category_key, status, created_at) VALUES "
        "(:original_name, :stored_name, :source_uri, 'image/jpeg', 1, :sha256, "
        ":category_key, 'ready', CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6))"
    )
    with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
        connection.execute(
            insert,
            {
                "original_name": "first.jpg",
                "stored_name": "first.jpg",
                "source_uri": source_uri,
                "sha256": "1" * 64,
                "category_key": "space_image",
            },
        )
        assert connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "SELECT CHAR_LENGTH(source_uri) FROM assets"
        ).scalar_one() == len(source_uri)

    with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
        connection.execute(
            insert,
            {
                "original_name": "second.jpg",
                "stored_name": "second.jpg",
                "source_uri": source_uri,
                "sha256": "2" * 64,
                "category_key": "knowledge_graph",
            },
        )

    with pytest.raises(IntegrityError):
        with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
            connection.execute(
                insert,
                {
                    "original_name": "third.jpg",
                    "stored_name": "third.jpg",
                    "source_uri": source_uri,
                    "sha256": "3" * 64,
                    "category_key": "space_image",
                },
            )


def test_mysql_schema_89_upgrades_global_source_uri_digest_to_category_scope(
    mysql_engine: Engine,
) -> None:
    with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
        create_mysql_baseline(connection, install_invariants=False)
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "ALTER TABLE `assets` DROP INDEX "
            "`uq_assets_source_uri_sha256_category`"
        )
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "CREATE UNIQUE INDEX `uq_assets_source_uri_sha256` "
            "ON `assets` (`source_uri_sha256`)"
        )
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "DELETE FROM schema_migrations WHERE version >= %s",
            (89,),
        )
        upgrade_mysql_schema(connection, expected=89)

        indexes = {
            item["name"]: tuple(item["column_names"])
            for item in inspect(connection).get_indexes("assets")
        }
        assert "uq_assets_source_uri_sha256" not in indexes
        assert indexes["uq_assets_source_uri_sha256_category"] == (
            "source_uri_sha256",
            "category_key",
        )
        assert connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "SELECT max(version) FROM schema_migrations"
        ).scalar_one() == 89


def test_mysql_schema_95_upgrades_foreach_and_invocation_identity(
    mysql_engine: Engine,
) -> None:
    with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
        create_mysql_baseline(connection, install_invariants=False)
        connection.exec_driver_sql("DROP TABLE `workflow_foreach_items`")
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "ALTER TABLE `workflow_model_invocations` DROP INDEX "
            "`uq_workflow_model_invocations_attempt_key`"
        )
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "ALTER TABLE `workflow_model_invocations` DROP COLUMN "
            "`invocation_key`"
        )
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "ALTER TABLE `workflow_model_invocations` ADD CONSTRAINT "
            "`uq_workflow_model_invocations_step_attempt` "
            "UNIQUE (`step_attempt_id`)"
        )
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "DELETE FROM schema_migrations WHERE version >= %s",
            (95,),
        )

        upgrade_mysql_schema(connection, expected=95)

        inspector = inspect(connection)
        assert "workflow_foreach_items" in inspector.get_table_names()
        assert "invocation_key" in {
            column["name"]
            for column in inspector.get_columns("workflow_model_invocations")
        }
        unique_constraints = {
            item["name"]: tuple(item["column_names"])
            for item in inspector.get_unique_constraints(
                "workflow_model_invocations"
            )
        }
        assert unique_constraints[
            "uq_workflow_model_invocations_attempt_key"
        ] == ("step_attempt_id", "invocation_key")
        assert "uq_workflow_model_invocations_step_attempt" not in unique_constraints
        assert connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "SELECT max(version) FROM schema_migrations"
        ).scalar_one() == 95


def test_mysql_schema_96_upgrades_frozen_tier_pricing_evidence(
    mysql_engine: Engine,
) -> None:
    with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
        create_mysql_baseline(connection, install_invariants=False)
        for table_name, constraint_names in (
            ("model_configs", ("ck_model_configs_pricing_contract",)),
            (
                "model_registry_entries",
                ("ck_model_registry_pricing_contract",),
            ),
            (
                "workflow_model_invocations",
                (
                    "ck_workflow_model_invocations_pricing_snapshot",
                    "ck_workflow_model_invocations_actual_cost",
                ),
            ),
        ):
            for constraint_name in constraint_names:
                connection.exec_driver_sql(
                    f"ALTER TABLE `{table_name}` DROP CHECK `{constraint_name}`"
                )
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "ALTER TABLE `model_configs` DROP COLUMN `pricing_contract_json`"
        )
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "ALTER TABLE `model_registry_entries` "
            "DROP COLUMN `pricing_contract_json`"
        )
        for column_name in (
            "pricing_snapshot_json",
            "pricing_tier_key",
            "actual_cost_micros",
        ):
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "ALTER TABLE `workflow_model_invocations` "
                f"DROP COLUMN `{column_name}`"
            )
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "DELETE FROM schema_migrations WHERE version >= %s",
            (96,),
        )

        upgrade_mysql_schema(connection, expected=96)

        assert "pricing_contract_json" in {
            column["name"]
            for column in inspect(connection).get_columns("model_configs")
        }
        assert "pricing_contract_json" in {
            column["name"]
            for column in inspect(connection).get_columns(
                "model_registry_entries"
            )
        }
        assert {
            "pricing_snapshot_json",
            "pricing_tier_key",
            "actual_cost_micros",
        } <= {
            column["name"]
            for column in inspect(connection).get_columns(
                "workflow_model_invocations"
            )
        }
        assert connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "SELECT max(version) FROM schema_migrations"
        ).scalar_one() == 96
        trigger_sql = connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "SELECT ACTION_STATEMENT FROM information_schema.TRIGGERS "
            "WHERE TRIGGER_SCHEMA = DATABASE() AND TRIGGER_NAME = %s",
            ("trg_workflow_model_invocations_identity_frozen",),
        ).scalar_one()
        assert "pricing_snapshot_json" in str(trigger_sql)

        connection.exec_driver_sql("SET FOREIGN_KEY_CHECKS=0")
        try:
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "INSERT INTO workflow_model_invocations ("
                "id, production_run_id, step_attempt_id, invocation_key, "
                "node_instance_id, status, provider, model_id, "
                "pricing_snapshot_json, pricing_tier_key, "
                "actual_cost_micros, evidence_json, created_at) VALUES ("
                "900096, 900096, 900096, 'root', 'prompt-a', 'reserved', "
                "'volcengine', 'doubao-seed', "
                "'{\"schema_version\":\"model_pricing_contract_v2\"}', "
                "'', 0, '{}', CURRENT_TIMESTAMP)"
            )
            with pytest.raises(DBAPIError):
                connection.exec_driver_sql(
                    "UPDATE workflow_model_invocations SET "
                    "pricing_snapshot_json='{\"schema_version\":\"tampered\"}' "
                    "WHERE id=900096"
                )

            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "UPDATE workflow_model_invocations SET "
                "status='succeeded', pricing_tier_key='input_0_32k', "
                "actual_cost_micros=1234 WHERE id=900096"
            )
            assert connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "SELECT status, pricing_tier_key, actual_cost_micros "
                "FROM workflow_model_invocations WHERE id=900096"
            ).one() == ("succeeded", "input_0_32k", 1234)
        finally:
            connection.exec_driver_sql("SET FOREIGN_KEY_CHECKS=1")


def test_mysql_schema_97_repairs_legacy_invocation_identity_trigger(
    mysql_engine: Engine,
) -> None:
    with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
        create_mysql_baseline(connection, install_invariants=False)
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "DELETE FROM schema_migrations WHERE version = %s",
            (97,),
        )
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS `trg_workflow_model_invocations_identity_frozen`"
        )
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "CREATE TRIGGER `trg_workflow_model_invocations_identity_frozen` "
            "BEFORE UPDATE ON `workflow_model_invocations` FOR EACH ROW "
            "BEGIN IF NOT ("
            "OLD.`pricing_snapshot_json` <=> NEW.`pricing_snapshot_json` AND "
            "OLD.`pricing_tier_key` <=> NEW.`pricing_tier_key`"
            ") THEN SIGNAL SQLSTATE '45000' SET MESSAGE_TEXT = "
            "'Workflow model invocation identity is immutable'; END IF; END"
        )

        upgrade_mysql_schema(connection, expected=97)

        assert connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "SELECT max(version) FROM schema_migrations"
        ).scalar_one() == 97
        trigger_sql = str(
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "SELECT ACTION_STATEMENT FROM information_schema.TRIGGERS "
                "WHERE TRIGGER_SCHEMA = DATABASE() AND TRIGGER_NAME = %s",
                ("trg_workflow_model_invocations_identity_frozen",),
            ).scalar_one()
        )
        assert "pricing_snapshot_json" in trigger_sql
        assert "pricing_tier_key" not in trigger_sql

        connection.exec_driver_sql("SET FOREIGN_KEY_CHECKS=0")
        try:
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "INSERT INTO workflow_model_invocations ("
                "id, production_run_id, step_attempt_id, invocation_key, "
                "node_instance_id, status, provider, model_id, "
                "pricing_snapshot_json, pricing_tier_key, "
                "actual_cost_micros, evidence_json, created_at) VALUES ("
                "900097, 900097, 900097, 'root', 'prompt-a', 'reserved', "
                "'volcengine', 'doubao-seed', "
                "'{\"schema_version\":\"model_pricing_contract_v2\"}', "
                "'', 0, '{}', CURRENT_TIMESTAMP)"
            )
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "UPDATE workflow_model_invocations SET "
                "status='succeeded', pricing_tier_key='input_0_32k', "
                "actual_cost_micros=1234 WHERE id=900097"
            )
            with pytest.raises(DBAPIError):
                connection.exec_driver_sql(
                    "UPDATE workflow_model_invocations SET "
                    "pricing_snapshot_json='{\"schema_version\":\"tampered\"}' "
                    "WHERE id=900097"
                )
        finally:
            connection.exec_driver_sql("SET FOREIGN_KEY_CHECKS=1")


def test_mysql_schema_fingerprint_is_stable_and_version_guard_fails_closed(
    mysql_engine: Engine,
) -> None:
    with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
        create_mysql_baseline(connection, install_invariants=False)
        first = mysql_schema_fingerprint(connection)
        second = mysql_schema_fingerprint(connection)
        assert_mysql_schema_version(connection, expected=LATEST_SCHEMA_VERSION)
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "DELETE FROM schema_migrations WHERE version = %s",
            (LATEST_SCHEMA_VERSION,),
        )

    assert re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{64}", first)
    assert second == first
    with mysql_engine.connect() as connection:
        with pytest.raises(
            MySQLBaselineError,
            match=rf"期望 {LATEST_SCHEMA_VERSION}",
        ):
            assert_mysql_schema_version(
                connection,
                expected=LATEST_SCHEMA_VERSION,
            )

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
import stat
from pathlib import Path
from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import URL

from app.mysql_schema_clone import (
    CandidateDatabaseAccounts,
    MySQLSchemaCloneError,
    MySQLSchemaEvidence,
    MySQLTableEvidence,
    assert_exact_schema_clone,
    candidate_identity_names,
    clone_schema_with_clients,
    mysql_schema_evidence,
    prepare_candidate_database,
    write_candidate_runtime_dsns,

def _write_executable(path: Path, source: str) -> Path:
    path.write_text(source, encoding="utf-8")
    path.chmod(path.stat().st_mode | stat.S_IXUSR)
    return path


def test_candidate_runtime_dsns_stay_private_and_off_external_volumes(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    accounts = CandidateDatabaseAccounts(
        app_username="ls_app_1234567890ab",
        app_password="fixture-app-secret",
        migration_username="ls_mig_1234567890ab",
        migration_password="fixture-migration-secret",
    )
    private_root = tmp_path / "runtime-secrets"

    files = write_candidate_runtime_dsns(
        private_root,
        schema="labelsystem_docker_candidate_20260829_04c8298",
        host="host.docker.internal",
        port=33084,
        accounts=accounts,
    )

    assert private_root.stat().st_mode & 0o777 == 0o700
    assert files.app.stat().st_mode & 0o777 == 0o600
    assert files.migration.stat().st_mode & 0o777 == 0o600
    app_url = URL.create("sqlite")
    app_url = app_url.set(
        drivername="mysql+pymysql",
        username="ls_app_1234567890ab",
        password="fixture-app-secret",
        host="host.docker.internal",
        port=33084,
        database="labelsystem_docker_candidate_20260829_04c8298",
        query={"charset": "utf8mb4"},
    )
    assert files.app.read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip() == app_url.render_as_string(
        hide_password=False
    )
    assert "fixture-app-secret" not in repr(files)
    assert "fixture-migration-secret" not in repr(files)

    with pytest.raises(MySQLSchemaCloneError, match="外置卷"):
        write_candidate_runtime_dsns(
            Path("/Volumes/WorkSSD/forbidden-candidate-secrets"),
            schema="labelsystem_docker_candidate_20260829_04c8298",
            host="host.docker.internal",
            port=33084,
            accounts=accounts,
        )


def test_clone_streams_between_real_client_processes_without_passwords_in_argv(
    tmp_path: Path,
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    trace_path = tmp_path / "trace.jsonl"
    monkeypatch.setenv("MYSQL_CLONE_TEST_TRACE", str(trace_path))
    dump_client = _write_executable(
        tmp_path / "mysqldump",
        """#!/usr/bin/env python3
import json, os, pathlib, stat, sys
option_arg = next(value for value in sys.argv[1:] if value.startswith('--defaults-extra-file='))
option_file = pathlib.Path(option_arg.split('=', 1)[1])
payload = option_file.read_text(encoding='utf-8')
assert option_file.stat().st_mode & 0o777 == 0o600
assert 'source-fixture-secret' in payload
assert all('source-fixture-secret' not in value for value in sys.argv)
with pathlib.Path(os.environ['MYSQL_CLONE_TEST_TRACE']).open('a', encoding='utf-8') as output:
    output.write(json.dumps({'kind': 'dump', 'argv': sys.argv[1:], 'option': str(option_file)}) + '\\n')
sys.stdout.write('/*!50003 CREATE*/ /*!50017 DEFINER=`source-user`@`localhost`*/ /*!50003 TRIGGER `clone_probe_trigger` BEFORE UPDATE ON `clone_probe` FOR EACH ROW SET NEW.id=OLD.id */;;\\n')
sys.stdout.write('CREATE TABLE clone_probe(id INTEGER);\\nINSERT INTO clone_probe VALUES (1);\\n')
""",
    )
    restore_client = _write_executable(
        tmp_path / "mysql",
        """#!/usr/bin/env python3
import json, os, pathlib, sys
option_arg = next(value for value in sys.argv[1:] if value.startswith('--defaults-extra-file='))
option_file = pathlib.Path(option_arg.split('=', 1)[1])
payload = option_file.read_text(encoding='utf-8')
assert option_file.stat().st_mode & 0o777 == 0o600
assert 'target-fixture-secret' in payload
assert all('target-fixture-secret' not in value for value in sys.argv)
stream = sys.stdin.read()
assert 'CREATE TABLE clone_probe' in stream
assert 'clone_probe_trigger' in stream
assert 'DEFINER=' not in stream
with pathlib.Path(os.environ['MYSQL_CLONE_TEST_TRACE']).open('a', encoding='utf-8') as output:
    output.write(json.dumps({'kind': 'restore', 'argv': sys.argv[1:], 'option': str(option_file)}) + '\\n')
""",
    )
    source_url = URL.create(
        "mysql+pymysql",
        username="source-user",
        password="source-fixture-secret",
        host="localhost",
        port=33084,
        database="source_schema",
        query={"unix_socket": "/tmp/source.sock", "charset": "utf8mb4"},
    )
    target_url = URL.create(
        "mysql+pymysql",
        username="target-user",
        password="target-fixture-secret",
        host="localhost",
        port=33084,
        database="target_schema",
        query={"unix_socket": "/tmp/target.sock", "charset": "utf8mb4"},
    )

    report = clone_schema_with_clients(
        source_url=source_url,
        target_url=target_url,
        source_schema="source_schema",
        target_schema="target_schema",
        mysqldump=dump_client,
        mysql=restore_client,
    )

    trace = [json.loads(line) for line in trace_path.read_text().splitlines()]
    assert report.source_schema == "source_schema"
    assert report.target_schema == "target_schema"
    assert report.stripped_definers == 1
    assert [entry["kind"] for entry in trace] == ["dump", "restore"]
    assert all(
        "source-fixture-secret" not in json.dumps(entry)
        and "target-fixture-secret" not in json.dumps(entry)
        for entry in trace
    )
    assert all(not Path(entry["option"]).exists() for entry in trace)


def test_clone_failure_is_generic_and_cleans_temporary_option_files(
    tmp_path: Path,
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    trace_path = tmp_path / "failure-trace.json"
    monkeypatch.setenv("MYSQL_CLONE_TEST_TRACE", str(trace_path))
    dump_client = _write_executable(
        tmp_path / "mysqldump",
        """#!/usr/bin/env python3
import json, os, pathlib, sys
option = next(value.split('=', 1)[1] for value in sys.argv[1:] if value.startswith('--defaults-extra-file='))
pathlib.Path(os.environ['MYSQL_CLONE_TEST_TRACE']).write_text(json.dumps({'dump': option}), encoding='utf-8')
sys.stdout.write('SELECT 1;\\n')
""",
    )
    restore_client = _write_executable(
        tmp_path / "mysql",
        """#!/usr/bin/env python3
import json, os, pathlib, sys
trace = pathlib.Path(os.environ['MYSQL_CLONE_TEST_TRACE'])
payload = json.loads(trace.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
payload['restore'] = next(value.split('=', 1)[1] for value in sys.argv[1:] if value.startswith('--defaults-extra-file='))
trace.write_text(json.dumps(payload), encoding='utf-8')
sys.stdin.read()
raise SystemExit(7)
""",
    )
    source_url = URL.create(
        "mysql+pymysql",
        username="source-user",
        password="source-fixture-secret",
        database="source_schema",
    )
    target_url = URL.create(
        "mysql+pymysql",
        username="target-user",
        password="target-fixture-secret",
        database="target_schema",
    )

    with pytest.raises(MySQLSchemaCloneError, match="导入失败") as captured:
        clone_schema_with_clients(
            source_url=source_url,
            target_url=target_url,
            source_schema="source_schema",
            target_schema="target_schema",
            mysqldump=dump_client,
            mysql=restore_client,
        )

    assert "fixture-secret" not in str(captured.value)
    trace = json.loads(trace_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    assert not Path(trace["dump"]).exists()
    assert not Path(trace["restore"]).exists()


def test_restore_failure_is_not_misreported_as_source_broken_pipe(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    dump_client = _write_executable(
        tmp_path / "mysqldump",
        """#!/usr/bin/env python3
import sys
chunk = b'x' * 65536
for _index in range(1024):
    sys.stdout.buffer.write(chunk)
    sys.stdout.buffer.flush()
""",
    )
    restore_client = _write_executable(
        tmp_path / "mysql",
        """#!/usr/bin/env python3
import sys
sys.stderr.write('fixture target rejected import')
raise SystemExit(9)
""",
    )
    source_url = URL.create(
        "mysql+pymysql",
        username="source-user",
        password="source-fixture-secret",
        database="source_schema",
    )
    target_url = URL.create(
        "mysql+pymysql",
        username="target-user",
        password="target-fixture-secret",
        database="target_schema",
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        MySQLSchemaCloneError,
        match="目标 schema 导入失败",
    ) as captured:
        clone_schema_with_clients(
            source_url=source_url,
            target_url=target_url,
            source_schema="source_schema",
            target_schema="target_schema",
            mysqldump=dump_client,
            mysql=restore_client,
        )

    assert "fixture target rejected import" in str(captured.value)
    assert "fixture-secret" not in str(captured.value)
