from __future__ import annotations

import json

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, func, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session, sessionmaker

from app.audit import canonical_json
from app.database import Base
from app.models import (
    Asset,
    BaselineRegressionItem,
    BaselineRegressionRun,
    BaselineSet,
    BaselineSetItem,
    EvaluationJob,
    EvaluationResult,
    ProductionRun,
    ProductionStepAttempt,
)

from test_stock_mechanism_selection import _mechanism


def _baseline(db: Session, *, field_truth: bool = False) -> BaselineSet:
    asset = Asset(
        original_name="native-stock.jpg",
        stored_name="native-stock.jpg",
        mime_type="image/jpeg",
        size_bytes=20,
        sha256="f" * 64,
        category_key="space_image",
    )
    db.add(asset)
    baseline = BaselineSet(
        category_key="space_image",
        name="原生存量回归测试集",
        description="",
        default_expected_level="L1",
        fingerprint="a" * 64,
        created_by="ops",
    )
    db.add(baseline)
    db.flush()
    db.add(
        BaselineSetItem(
            baseline_set_id=baseline.id,
            asset_id=asset.id,
            expected_level=None if field_truth else "L2",
            expected_result_json=(
                canonical_json({"style": "现代", "is_single": 0})
                if field_truth
                else canonical_json({"level": "L2"})
            ),
            truth_contract_key=("kg.fields.v1" if field_truth else "level.v1"),
            asset_snapshot_json=canonical_json(
                {"asset_id": asset.id, "sha256": asset.sha256}
            ),
        )
    )
    db.flush()
    return baseline


def _add_level_truth_item(
    db: Session,
    baseline: BaselineSet,
    *,
    suffix: str,
) -> BaselineSetItem:
    asset = Asset(
        original_name=f"native-stock-{suffix}.jpg",
        stored_name=f"native-stock-{suffix}.jpg",
        mime_type="image/jpeg",
        size_bytes=21,
        sha256=(suffix[-1] if suffix[-1].isdigit() else "e") * 64,
        category_key=baseline.category_key,
    )
    db.add(asset)
    db.flush()
    item = BaselineSetItem(
        baseline_set_id=baseline.id,
        asset_id=asset.id,
        expected_level="L2",
        expected_result_json=canonical_json({"level": "L2"}),
        truth_contract_key="level.v1",
        asset_snapshot_json=canonical_json(
            {"asset_id": asset.id, "sha256": asset.sha256}
        ),
    )
    db.add(item)
    db.flush()
    return item


def test_create_native_baseline_run_creates_only_workflow_runs(tmp_path) -> None:
    from app.native_baseline_regression import create_native_baseline_run

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'native-stock-create.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            baseline = _baseline(db)
            workflow = _mechanism(db)

            run = create_native_baseline_run(
                db,
                baseline_set=baseline,
                workflow_version=workflow,
                actor="ops",
                idempotency_key="native-stock-run-1",
            )
            duplicate = create_native_baseline_run(
                db,
                baseline_set=baseline,
                workflow_version=workflow,
                actor="ops",
                idempotency_key="native-stock-run-1",
            )
            db.flush()

            assert duplicate.id == run.id
            assert run.execution_engine == "native_workflow"
            assert run.workflow_version_id == workflow.id
            assert run.strategy_bundle_id is None
            assert len(run.items) == 1
            assert run.items[0].expected_result_json == canonical_json(
                {"level": "L2"}
            )
            assert run.items[0].truth_contract_key == "level.v1"
            assert run.items[0].production_run_id is not None
            assert db.scalar(select(func.count(ProductionRun.id))) == 1
            assert db.scalar(select(func.count(EvaluationJob.id))) == 0

            production = db.get(ProductionRun, run.items[0].production_run_id)
            assert production is not None
            frozen = json.loads(production.snapshot_json)
            assert frozen["workflow"]["workflow_version_id"] == workflow.id
            assert frozen["workflow"]["canonical_hash"] == workflow.canonical_hash
            assert frozen["runtime_context"]["baseline_run_id"] == run.id
            assert frozen["runtime_context"]["baseline_item_id"] == run.items[0].id
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_failed_native_children_terminalize_the_parent(tmp_path) -> None:
    from app.native_baseline_regression import create_native_baseline_run
    from app.workflow_runtime import terminalize_runtime_run

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'native-failed-parent.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            baseline = _baseline(db)
            _add_level_truth_item(db, baseline, suffix="2")
            workflow = _mechanism(db)
            run = create_native_baseline_run(
                db,
                baseline_set=baseline,
                workflow_version=workflow,
                actor="ops",
                idempotency_key="native-failed-parent",
            )

            for item in run.items:
                production = db.get(ProductionRun, item.production_run_id)
                assert production is not None
                terminalize_runtime_run(
                    db,
                    production,
                    status="failed",
                    error_code="model_provider_unavailable",
                    error_message="provider detail must-not-reach-baseline",
                )
                terminalize_runtime_run(
                    db,
                    production,
                    status="failed",
                    error_code="model_provider_unavailable",
                    error_message="provider detail must-not-reach-baseline",
                )

            db.flush()
            assert [item.status for item in run.items] == ["failed", "failed"]
            assert run.status == "failed"
            assert run.failed == 2
            assert run.completed == 2
            assert run.valid_predictions == 0
            assert run.finished_at is not None
            assert all(
                "must-not-reach-baseline" not in item.result_snapshot_json
                for item in run.items
            )
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_manual_runtime_retry_reopens_failed_native_baseline_item(tmp_path) -> None:
    from app.native_baseline_regression import create_native_baseline_run
    from app.workflow_runtime import (
        retry_failed_run_from_checkpoint,
        terminalize_runtime_run,
    )

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'native-manual-retry.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            baseline = _baseline(db)
            workflow = _mechanism(db)
            baseline_run = create_native_baseline_run(
                db,
                baseline_set=baseline,
                workflow_version=workflow,
                actor="ops",
                idempotency_key="native-manual-retry",
            )
            item = baseline_run.items[0]
            production = db.get(ProductionRun, item.production_run_id)
            assert production is not None
            first_attempt = db.scalar(
                select(ProductionStepAttempt)
                .where(ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == production.id)
                .order_by(ProductionStepAttempt.sequence)
            )
            assert first_attempt is not None
            first_attempt.status = "failed"
            first_attempt.last_error_code = "prompt_placeholder_unresolved"
            first_attempt.last_error_message = "frozen prompt was incomplete"
            terminalize_runtime_run(
                db,
                production,
                status="failed",
                error_code="prompt_placeholder_unresolved",
                error_message="frozen prompt was incomplete",
            )
            assert item.status == "failed"
            assert baseline_run.status == "failed"

            retry_failed_run_from_checkpoint(db, production.id)

            assert item.status == "queued"
            assert item.result_snapshot_json == "{}"
            assert item.error_message == ""
            assert item.finished_at is None
            assert baseline_run.status == "running"
            assert baseline_run.failed == 0
            assert baseline_run.finished_at is None
    finally:
        engine.dispose()

def test_terminal_child_recovery_locks_run_before_baseline_item(tmp_path) -> None:
    """MySQL workers must not invert the normal run -> item lock order."""

    from datetime import datetime, timezone

    from sqlalchemy import event

    from app.native_baseline_regression import (
        create_native_baseline_run,
        reconcile_terminal_native_baseline_children,
    )

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'native-lock-order.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    statements: list[str] = []

    def record_statement(
        _connection,
        _cursor,
        statement,
        _parameters,
        _context,
        _executemany,
    ) -> None:
        statements.append(" ".join(statement.lower().split()))

    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            baseline = _baseline(db)
            workflow = _mechanism(db)
            run = create_native_baseline_run(
                db,
                baseline_set=baseline,
                workflow_version=workflow,
                actor="ops",
                idempotency_key="native-lock-order",
            )
            production = db.get(
                ProductionRun,
                run.items[0].production_run_id,
            )
            assert production is not None
            production.status = "failed"
            production.error_code = "model_execution_unexpected"
            production.finished_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
            db.flush()

            event.listen(engine, "before_cursor_execute", record_statement)
            try:
                assert reconcile_terminal_native_baseline_children(db) == 1
            finally:
                event.remove(engine, "before_cursor_execute", record_statement)

            candidate_index = next(
                index
                for index, statement in enumerate(statements)
                if statement.startswith(
                    "select production_runs.id from production_runs join"
                )
            )
            run_lock_index = next(
                index
                for index, statement in enumerate(statements)
                if statement.startswith("select production_runs.id,")
                and "where production_runs.id =" in statement
            )
            item_lock_index = next(
                index
                for index, statement in enumerate(statements)
                if statement.startswith("select baseline_regression_items.id,")
                and "where baseline_regression_items.id =" in statement
            )
            assert candidate_index < run_lock_index < item_lock_index
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_success_finalizer_locks_parent_before_baseline_item(tmp_path) -> None:
    """Parallel child completion must serialize through the parent run lock."""

    from sqlalchemy import event

    from app.native_baseline_regression import (
        create_native_baseline_run,
        finalize_native_baseline_item,
    )
    from app.workflow_result_finalizer import NativeEvaluationFact

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'native-parent-lock-order.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    statements: list[str] = []

    def record_statement(
        _connection,
        _cursor,
        statement,
        _parameters,
        _context,
        _executemany,
    ) -> None:
        statements.append(" ".join(statement.lower().split()))

    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            baseline = _baseline(db)
            workflow = _mechanism(db)
            run = create_native_baseline_run(
                db,
                baseline_set=baseline,
                workflow_version=workflow,
                actor="ops",
                idempotency_key="native-parent-lock-order",
            )
            production = db.get(ProductionRun, run.items[0].production_run_id)
            assert production is not None
            production.status = "succeeded"
            db.flush()

            event.listen(engine, "before_cursor_execute", record_statement)
            try:
                finalize_native_baseline_item(
                    db,
                    production,
                    NativeEvaluationFact(
                        normalized_result={"level": "L2"},
                        result_hash="b" * 64,
                        workflow_version_id=workflow.id,
                        run_id=production.id,
                        node_outputs={"final": {"status": "succeeded"}},
                        evaluation_job_id=201,
                        evaluation_result_id=202,
                    ),
                )
            finally:
                event.remove(engine, "before_cursor_execute", record_statement)

            parent_lock_index = next(
                index
                for index, statement in enumerate(statements)
                if statement.startswith("select baseline_regression_runs.id,")
                and "where baseline_regression_runs.id =" in statement
            )
            item_lock_index = next(
                index
                for index, statement in enumerate(statements)
                if statement.startswith("select baseline_regression_items.id,")
                and "where baseline_regression_items.id =" in statement
            )
            assert parent_lock_index < item_lock_index
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_runtime_output_persistence_locks_parent_before_baseline_item(tmp_path) -> None:
    """The pre-finalizer snapshot write must follow the same parent-first order."""

    from sqlalchemy import event

    from app.native_baseline_regression import create_native_baseline_run
    from app.workflow_runtime import persist_baseline_workflow_outputs

    engine = create_engine(
        f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'native-runtime-output-lock-order.db'}"
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    statements: list[str] = []

    def record_statement(
        _connection,
        _cursor,
        statement,
        _parameters,
        _context,
        _executemany,
    ) -> None:
        statements.append(" ".join(statement.lower().split()))

    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            baseline = _baseline(db)
            workflow = _mechanism(db)
            run = create_native_baseline_run(
                db,
                baseline_set=baseline,
                workflow_version=workflow,
                actor="ops",
                idempotency_key="native-runtime-output-lock-order",
            )
            production = db.get(ProductionRun, run.items[0].production_run_id)
            assert production is not None
            production.status = "succeeded"
            db.flush()

            event.listen(engine, "before_cursor_execute", record_statement)
            try:
                persist_baseline_workflow_outputs(db, production)
            finally:
                event.remove(engine, "before_cursor_execute", record_statement)

            parent_lock_index = next(
                index
                for index, statement in enumerate(statements)
                if statement.startswith("select baseline_regression_runs.id,")
                and "where baseline_regression_runs.id =" in statement
            )
            item_lock_index = next(
                index
                for index, statement in enumerate(statements)
                if statement.startswith("select baseline_regression_items.id,")
                and "where baseline_regression_items.id =" in statement
            )
            assert parent_lock_index < item_lock_index
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_recovery_repairs_terminal_children_with_stale_parent_counters(
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    from app.native_baseline_regression import (
        create_native_baseline_run,
        reconcile_stale_native_baseline_parents,
    )

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'native-stale-counters.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            baseline = _baseline(db)
            _add_level_truth_item(db, baseline, suffix="2")
            workflow = _mechanism(db)
            run = create_native_baseline_run(
                db,
                baseline_set=baseline,
                workflow_version=workflow,
                actor="ops",
                idempotency_key="native-stale-counters",
            )
            for item in run.items:
                item.status = "completed"
                item.result_snapshot_json = canonical_json(
                    {"normalized_result": {"level": "L2"}}
                )
            run.status = "running"
            run.completed = 0
            run.valid_predictions = 0
            run.failed = 0
            run.metrics_json = "{}"
            db.flush()

            assert reconcile_stale_native_baseline_parents(db) == 1
            assert reconcile_stale_native_baseline_parents(db) == 0
            assert run.status == "completed"
            assert run.completed == 2
            assert run.valid_predictions == 2
            assert run.failed == 0
            assert run.finished_at is not None
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_mixed_native_children_terminalize_as_partial_failed(tmp_path) -> None:
    from app.native_baseline_regression import (
        create_native_baseline_run,
        finalize_native_baseline_item,
    )
    from app.workflow_result_finalizer import NativeEvaluationFact
    from app.workflow_runtime import terminalize_runtime_run

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'native-mixed-parent.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            baseline = _baseline(db)
            _add_level_truth_item(db, baseline, suffix="2")
            workflow = _mechanism(db)
            run = create_native_baseline_run(
                db,
                baseline_set=baseline,
                workflow_version=workflow,
                actor="ops",
                idempotency_key="native-mixed-parent",
            )
            first, second = run.items
            successful = db.get(ProductionRun, first.production_run_id)
            failed = db.get(ProductionRun, second.production_run_id)
            assert successful is not None and failed is not None
            successful.status = "succeeded"
            finalize_native_baseline_item(
                db,
                successful,
                NativeEvaluationFact(
                    normalized_result={"level": "L2"},
                    result_hash="b" * 64,
                    workflow_version_id=workflow.id,
                    run_id=successful.id,
                    node_outputs={"final": {"status": "succeeded"}},
                    evaluation_job_id=201,
                    evaluation_result_id=202,
                ),
            )
            terminalize_runtime_run(
                db,
                failed,
                status="failed",
                error_code="model_provider_timeout",
                error_message="provider detail",
            )

            db.flush()
            assert first.status == "completed"
            assert json.loads(first.result_snapshot_json)["normalized_result"] == {
                "level": "L2"
            }
            assert second.status == "failed"
            assert run.status == "partial_failed"
            assert run.completed == 2
            assert run.failed == 1
            assert run.valid_predictions == 1
            assert run.finished_at is not None
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_native_baseline_idempotency_rejects_truth_or_workflow_drift(
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    from app.native_baseline_regression import (
        NativeBaselineRegressionError,
        create_native_baseline_run,
    )

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'native-stock-drift.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            baseline = _baseline(db)
            first_item = baseline.items[0]
            second_asset = Asset(
                original_name="native-stock-2.jpg",
                stored_name="native-stock-2.jpg",
                mime_type="image/jpeg",
                size_bytes=21,
                sha256="e" * 64,
                category_key="space_image",
            )
            db.add(second_asset)
            db.flush()
            second_item = BaselineSetItem(
                baseline_set_id=baseline.id,
                asset_id=second_asset.id,
                expected_level="L3",
                expected_result_json=canonical_json({"level": "L3"}),
                truth_contract_key="level.v1",
                asset_snapshot_json=canonical_json(
                    {"asset_id": second_asset.id, "sha256": second_asset.sha256}
                ),
            )
            db.add(second_item)
            workflow = _mechanism(db)
            db.flush()

            create_native_baseline_run(
                db,
                baseline_set=baseline,
                workflow_version=workflow,
                actor="ops",
                idempotency_key="native-stock-stable-key",
                items=[first_item],
            )

            with pytest.raises(NativeBaselineRegressionError) as truth_conflict:
                create_native_baseline_run(
                    db,
                    baseline_set=baseline,
                    workflow_version=workflow,
                    actor="ops",
                    idempotency_key="native-stock-stable-key",
                    items=[second_item],
                )
            assert truth_conflict.value.code == (
                "native_baseline_idempotency_conflict"
            )

            workflow.canonical_hash = "d" * 64
            db.flush()
            with pytest.raises(NativeBaselineRegressionError) as workflow_conflict:
                create_native_baseline_run(
                    db,
                    baseline_set=baseline,
                    workflow_version=workflow,
                    actor="ops",
                    idempotency_key="native-stock-stable-key",
                    items=[first_item],
                )
            assert workflow_conflict.value.code == (
                "native_baseline_idempotency_conflict"
            )
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_new_run_freezes_latest_truth_revision_without_changing_old_runs(
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    from app.baseline_truth import append_baseline_truth_revision
    from app.native_baseline_regression import create_native_baseline_run

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'native-latest-truth.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            baseline = _baseline(db)
            source_item = baseline.items[0]
            workflow = _mechanism(db)
            old_run = create_native_baseline_run(
                db,
                baseline_set=baseline,
                workflow_version=workflow,
                actor="ops",
                idempotency_key="native-truth-v0",
            )
            old_run.status = "completed"
            append_baseline_truth_revision(
                db,
                item=source_item,
                expected_level="L1",
                expected_result={"level": "L1"},
                truth_contract_key="level.v1",
                workflow_input={},
                source_baseline_run_id=old_run.id,
                source_production_run_id=None,
                source_correction_key="correction:level:1",
                reason="运营确认等级",
                actor="ops",
            )

            new_run = create_native_baseline_run(
                db,
                baseline_set=baseline,
                workflow_version=workflow,
                actor="ops",
                idempotency_key="native-truth-v1",
            )
            db.flush()

            assert source_item.expected_level == "L2"
            assert old_run.items[0].expected_level == "L2"
            assert json.loads(old_run.items[0].expected_result_json or "{}") == {
                "level": "L2"
            }
            assert new_run.items[0].expected_level == "L1"
            assert json.loads(new_run.items[0].expected_result_json or "{}") == {
                "level": "L1"
            }
            assert old_run.baseline_set_fingerprint != (
                new_run.baseline_set_fingerprint
            )
            frozen = json.loads(new_run.execution_snapshot_json)["truth_snapshot"][0]
            assert frozen["truth_source"] == "revision"
            assert frozen["truth_revision"] == 1
            assert frozen["truth_revision_id"] is not None
            assert len(frozen["truth_hash"]) == 64
            production = db.get(
                ProductionRun,
                new_run.items[0].production_run_id,
            )
            assert production is not None
            runtime_input = json.loads(production.snapshot_json)["runtime_context"][
                "input_manifest"
            ]
            assert runtime_input["expected_result"] == {"level": "L1"}
            assert runtime_input["truth_revision"] == 1
            assert runtime_input["truth_hash"] == frozen["truth_hash"]
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_field_truth_metrics_do_not_require_a_level() -> None:
    from app.native_baseline_regression import compute_truth_metrics

    metrics = compute_truth_metrics(
        [
            {
                "status": "completed",
                "expected_result": {"style": "现代", "is_single": 0},
                "predicted_result": {"style": "现代", "is_single": 0},
            },
            {
                "status": "completed",
                "expected_result": {"style": "自然", "is_single": 1},
                "predicted_result": {"style": "现代", "is_single": 1},
            },
            {
                "status": "manual_required",
                "expected_result": {"style": "工业", "is_single": 0},
                "predicted_result": None,
            },
        ]
    )

    assert metrics["total"] == 3
    assert metrics["valid_predictions"] == 2
    assert metrics["coverage"] == 2 / 3
    assert metrics["field_metrics"]["style"]["accuracy"] == 0.5
    assert metrics["field_metrics"]["is_single"]["accuracy"] == 1.0
    assert metrics["field_aggregate"] == {
        "compared": 2, "correct": 1, "accuracy": 0.5, "precision": 0.5,
        "recall": 0.5, "true_positive": 1, "false_positive": 1, "false_negative": 1,
    }
    assert metrics["identity_aggregate"]["accuracy"] == 1.0
    assert metrics["level_metrics"] is None


def test_frozen_field_contract_identities_include_foreach_wildcard() -> None:
    from app.native_baseline_regression import (
        field_contract_identities_from_snapshot,
    )

    strict = {
        "mode": "json_schema",
        "name": "case_image",
        "strict": True,
        "schema": {
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
                "score": {
                    "type": "integer",
                    "minimum": 0,
                    "maximum": 100,
                }
            },
            "required": ["score"],
            "additionalProperties": False,
        },
    }
    snapshot = {
        "workflow": {
            "version": "1",
            "manifest": {
                "schema_version": "workflow-v3",
                "category_key": "design_case",
                "nodes": [
                    {
                        "node_instance_id": "foreach_images",
                        "kind": "foreach_image",
                        "config": {"item_executor": {"output_contract": strict}},
                    }
                ],
                "edges": [],
            },
        }
    }

    identities = field_contract_identities_from_snapshot(snapshot)

    assert len(identities) == 1
    assert identities[0]["node_instance_id"] == "foreach_images"
    assert identities[0]["field_path"] == "score"
    assert identities[0]["item_key"] == "*"
    assert identities[0]["contract_field_key"] == (
        "workflow.foreach_images.items[*].result.score"
    )
    assert len(identities[0]["output_contract_hash"]) == 64


def test_finalize_native_baseline_item_persists_field_result_and_metrics(
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    from app.baseline_regression import build_baseline_field_metrics
    from app.native_baseline_regression import (
        create_native_baseline_run,
        finalize_native_baseline_item,
    )
    from app.workflow_result_finalizer import NativeEvaluationFact

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'native-stock-finalize.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            baseline = _baseline(db, field_truth=True)
            workflow = _mechanism(db)
            run = create_native_baseline_run(
                db,
                baseline_set=baseline,
                workflow_version=workflow,
                actor="ops",
                idempotency_key="native-stock-fields-1",
            )
            item = run.items[0]
            production = db.get(ProductionRun, item.production_run_id)
            assert production is not None
            fact = NativeEvaluationFact(
                normalized_result={"style": "现代", "is_single": 0},
                result_hash="b" * 64,
                workflow_version_id=workflow.id,
                run_id=production.id,
                node_outputs={"kg_prompt": {"status": "succeeded"}},
                evaluation_job_id=101,
                evaluation_result_id=102,
            )

            finalized = finalize_native_baseline_item(db, production, fact)
            db.flush()

            assert finalized.id == item.id
            assert finalized.status == "completed"
            assert finalized.expected_level is None
            assert finalized.job_id == 101
            assert finalized.evaluation_id == 102
            result = json.loads(finalized.result_snapshot_json)
            assert result["normalized_result"] == {
                "style": "现代",
                "is_single": 0,
            }
            assert result["workflow_version_id"] == workflow.id
            metrics = json.loads(run.metrics_json)
            assert metrics["valid_predictions"] == 1
            assert metrics["field_aggregate"]["accuracy"] == 1.0
            assert metrics["level_metrics"] is None
            assert run.status == "completed"

            evidence = build_baseline_field_metrics(db, run)
            evidence_by_field = {
                row["field_key"]: row for row in evidence["field_metrics"]
            }
            assert evidence_by_field["style"]["accuracy"] == 1.0
            assert evidence_by_field["is_single"]["accuracy"] == 1.0
            assert evidence["golden_failure_sample_ids"] == []
            assert evidence["versions"]["model"] == ["fixture-model"]
            assert evidence["versions"]["mechanism"]["strategy_bundle_id"] is None
            assert evidence["versions"]["mechanism"]["strategy_canonical_id"] is None
            assert evidence["versions"]["workflow"] == {
                "workflow_version_id": workflow.id,
                "version": workflow.version,
                "canonical_hash": workflow.canonical_hash,
                "schema_version": "workflow-v3",
            }
            assert evidence["versions"]["components"]["prompt_nodes"] == [
                {
                    "node_instance_id": "prompt_c",
                    "display_name": "知识图谱字段提取",
                    "asset_key": "prompt_c",
                    "version_id": 31,
                    "version": "component-v2",
                    "model_id": "fixture-model",
                    "max_tokens": 8192,
                    "thinking_level": "none",
                    "thinking_enabled": False,
                }
            ]
            assert evidence["versions"]["truth"]["baseline_set_id"] == baseline.id
            assert evidence["versions"]["truth"]["matched_asset_count"] == 1
            assert evidence["versions"]["truth"]["contract_keys"] == [
                "kg.fields.v1"
            ]
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_knowledge_graph_native_regression_runs_and_finalizes_without_level(
    monkeypatch, tmp_path
) -> None:
    from sqlalchemy import event

    from app.knowledge_graph_contracts import get_knowledge_graph_contract
    from app.native_baseline_regression import create_native_baseline_run
    from app.workflow_fixture_executor import process_runtime_step_once

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'native-stock-kg-e2e.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    SessionForTest = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    try:
        with SessionForTest() as db:
            asset = Asset(
                original_name="kg-model.skp",
                stored_name="kg-model.skp",
                mime_type="application/octet-stream",
                size_bytes=100,
                sha256="9" * 64,
                category_key="model_3d_su",
            )
            db.add(asset)
            baseline = BaselineSet(
                category_key="model_3d_su",
                name="知识图谱字段回归",
                description="",
                default_expected_level="L1",
                fingerprint="8" * 64,
                created_by="ops",
            )
            db.add(baseline)
            db.flush()
            db.add(
                BaselineSetItem(
                    baseline_set_id=baseline.id,
                    asset_id=asset.id,
                    expected_level=None,
                    expected_result_json=canonical_json(
                        {"ll_id": "ll-1", "is_single": 0}
                    ),
                    truth_contract_key="kg.model_3d_su.domestic.whole.v1",
                    asset_snapshot_json=canonical_json(
                        {"asset_id": asset.id, "sha256": asset.sha256}
                    ),
                )
            )
            workflow = _mechanism(db, category_key="model_3d_su")
            contract = get_knowledge_graph_contract(
                "kg.model_3d_su.domestic.whole.v1"
            )
            assert contract is not None
            nodes = json.loads(workflow.step_manifest_json)
            prompt = next(node for node in nodes if node["kind"] == "prompt")
            prompt["config"]["output_contract_key"] = contract.contract_key
            prompt["config"]["output_contract_snapshot"] = contract.snapshot()
            prompt["output_schema"] = contract.output_schema
            workflow.step_manifest_json = canonical_json(nodes)
            db.flush()

            run = create_native_baseline_run(
                db,
                baseline_set=baseline,
                workflow_version=workflow,
                actor="ops",
                idempotency_key="native-stock-kg-1",
            )
            run_id = run.id
            item_id = run.items[0].id
            db.commit()

        import app.workflow_fixture_executor as executor

        monkeypatch.setattr(executor, "SessionLocal", SessionForTest)
        statements: list[str] = []

        def record_statement(
            _connection,
            _cursor,
            statement,
            _parameters,
            _context,
            _executemany,
        ) -> None:
            statements.append(" ".join(statement.lower().split()))

        event.listen(engine, "before_cursor_execute", record_statement)
        try:
            assert process_runtime_step_once("native-stock-kg-worker") is True
            assert process_runtime_step_once("native-stock-kg-worker") is True
            assert process_runtime_step_once("native-stock-kg-worker") is True
            assert process_runtime_step_once("native-stock-kg-worker") is True
            assert process_runtime_step_once("native-stock-kg-worker") is False
        finally:
            event.remove(engine, "before_cursor_execute", record_statement)

        parent_lock_index = next(
            index
            for index, statement in enumerate(statements)
            if statement.startswith("select baseline_regression_runs.id,")
            and "where baseline_regression_runs.id =" in statement
        )
        evaluation_job_insert_index = next(
            index
            for index, statement in enumerate(statements)
            if statement.startswith("insert into evaluation_jobs")
        )
        assert parent_lock_index < evaluation_job_insert_index

        with SessionForTest() as db:
            run = db.get(BaselineRegressionRun, run_id)
            item = db.get(BaselineRegressionItem, item_id)
            assert run is not None and item is not None
            assert run.status == "completed"
            assert run.valid_predictions == 1
            assert item.status == "completed"
            assert item.expected_level is None
            normalized = json.loads(item.result_snapshot_json)[
                "normalized_result"
            ]
            assert normalized["ll_id"] == "ll-1"
            assert normalized["is_single"] == 0
            assert "level" not in normalized
            assert db.scalar(select(func.count(EvaluationJob.id))) == 1
            assert db.scalar(select(func.count(EvaluationResult.id))) == 1
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_four_route_knowledge_graph_baseline_uses_frozen_workflow_inputs(
    monkeypatch, tmp_path
) -> None:
    from app.knowledge_graph_contracts import resolve_knowledge_graph_fixture
    from app.native_baseline_regression import create_native_baseline_run
    from app.native_workflow_migration import ensure_knowledge_graph_workflow
    from app.seed import seed_defaults
    from app.workflow_fixture_executor import process_runtime_step_once

    routes = (
        ("domestic", 0, "ll_id", "ll-baseline-whole"),
        ("domestic", 1, "ll_id", "ll-baseline-single"),
        ("overseas", 0, "res_id", "res-baseline-whole"),
        ("overseas", 1, "res_id", "res-baseline-single"),
    )
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'native-stock-kg-routes.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    sessions = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    try:
        with sessions() as db:
            seed_defaults(db)
            workflow = ensure_knowledge_graph_workflow(
                db,
                actor="system:kg-baseline-test",
                category_key="knowledge_graph",
            )
            baseline = BaselineSet(
                category_key="knowledge_graph",
                name="知识图谱四路字段回归",
                description="",
                default_expected_level="L1",
                fingerprint="5" * 64,
                created_by="ops",
            )
            db.add(baseline)
            db.flush()
            for index, (source, is_single, identity_field, identity) in enumerate(
                routes, start=1
            ):
                asset_scope = "single" if is_single else "whole"
                fixture_key = f"kg.{source}.{asset_scope}.v1"
                contract_key = f"kg.knowledge_graph.{source}.{asset_scope}.v1"
                expected = resolve_knowledge_graph_fixture(
                    fixture_key,
                    contract_key,
                    expected_asset_id=identity,
                )
                asset = Asset(
                    original_name=f"kg-route-{index}.skp",
                    stored_name=f"kg-route-{index}.skp",
                    mime_type="application/octet-stream",
                    size_bytes=100 + index,
                    sha256=f"{index}" * 64,
                    category_key="knowledge_graph",
                )
                db.add(asset)
                db.flush()
                db.add(
                    BaselineSetItem(
                        baseline_set_id=baseline.id,
                        asset_id=asset.id,
                        expected_level=None,
                        expected_result_json=canonical_json(expected),
                        truth_contract_key=contract_key,
                        asset_snapshot_json=canonical_json(
                            {
                                "asset_id": asset.id,
                                "sha256": asset.sha256,
                                "workflow_input": {
                                    "source": source,
                                    "is_single": is_single,
                                    identity_field: identity,
                                    "fixture_key": fixture_key,
                                },
                            }
                        ),
                    )
                )
            db.flush()
            run = create_native_baseline_run(
                db,
                baseline_set=baseline,
                workflow_version=workflow,
                actor="ops",
                idempotency_key="native-stock-kg-four-routes",
            )
            run_id = run.id
            db.commit()

        import app.workflow_fixture_executor as executor

        monkeypatch.setattr(executor, "SessionLocal", sessions)
        processed = 0
        while process_runtime_step_once("native-stock-kg-route-worker"):
            processed += 1
            assert processed <= 24

        with sessions() as db:
            run = db.get(BaselineRegressionRun, run_id)
            assert run is not None
            assert run.status == "completed"
            assert run.valid_predictions == 4
            assert all(item.status == "completed" for item in run.items)
            metrics = json.loads(run.metrics_json)
            assert metrics["coverage"] == 1.0
            assert metrics["field_aggregate"]["accuracy"] == 1.0
            assert metrics["level_metrics"] is None
            assert {
                json.loads(item.result_snapshot_json)["normalized_result"][
                    "is_single"
                ]
                for item in run.items
            } == {0, 1, 2}  # 国内 0/1 + 海外 1/2
    finally:
        engine.dispose()

"""旧豆包灵感图链只出 0-100 分，回归不再对它要求 level（L1-L5 由下游阈值映射）。"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from types import SimpleNamespace

from app.native_baseline_regression import _is_score_only_legacy_chain


def _run(policy: dict | None) -> SimpleNamespace:
    manifest = {"category_key": "inspiration_image", "nodes": []}
    if policy is not None:
        manifest["resource_policy"] = policy
    return SimpleNamespace(snapshot_json=json.dumps({"workflow": {"manifest": manifest}}))


def test_documented_php_chain_is_score_only() -> None:
    assert _is_score_only_legacy_chain(_run({"legacy_semantics": "documented_php_chain"}))
    assert not _is_score_only_legacy_chain(_run({"legacy_semantics": "other"}))
    assert not _is_score_only_legacy_chain(_run(None))
    assert not _is_score_only_legacy_chain(SimpleNamespace(snapshot_json="not json"))

"""Safety and actual component workload contracts for the isolated load probe."""
import importlib.util
import asyncio
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

SCRIPT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / 'scripts/probe-native-batch-capacity.py'


def module():
    assert SCRIPT.exists(), 'isolated capacity probe must exist'
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('capacity_probe', SCRIPT)
    result = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    spec.loader.exec_module(result)
    return result


@pytest.mark.parametrize('options', [
    {'slots': 0}, {'slots': 4097}, {'requests': 100001},
    {'wait_ms': 300001}, {'phase_threads': 9}, {'max_rss_mib': 1},
])
def test_rejects_unbounded_work_before_opening_resources(options):
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        module().validate_options(**dict(dict(slots=4, requests=8, wait_ms=10,
            phase_threads=2, max_rss_mib=1024), **options))


def test_no_remote_socket_can_escape_guard():
    import socket
    probe = module()
    with pytest.raises(PermissionError):
        probe.check_network_event('socket.connect', (None, ('8.8.8.8', 443)))
    with pytest.raises(PermissionError):
        probe.check_network_event('socket.getaddrinfo', ('example.com', 443, 0, 0, 0))
    probe.check_network_event('socket.connect', (None, ('127.0.0.1', 12345)))


def test_real_image_http_and_sqlite_workload_is_accounted(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setenv('DATABASE_URL', 'mysql+pymysql://do-not-use:secret@production/real')
    probe = module()
    # ru_maxrss includes all earlier tests; workload correctness needs a stable
    # resource sample. The dedicated guard test exercises the pressure path.
    monkeypatch.setattr(probe, 'rss_mib', lambda: 128)
    monkeypatch.setattr(probe, 'available_memory_mib', lambda: 4096)
    result = asyncio.run(probe.probe(slots=4, requests=9, wait_ms=35,
        phase_threads=2, max_rss_mib=2048, burst=True, warmup=2))
    assert result['stop_reason'] is None, result
    assert result['evidence_kind'] == 'synthetic_runtime_components'
    assert result['native_workflow_e2e'] is False
    assert result['completed'] == result['requested'] == 9
    assert result['failures'] == result['duplicates'] == result['external_requests'] == 0
    assert result['image_preparations'] == result['db_commits'] == 9
    assert result['peak_http_inflight'] == 4
    assert result['heartbeat_count'] > 0
    assert result['warmup_completed'] == 2
    assert result['database_removed'] is True
    assert result['drained'] is True


def test_resource_guard_stops_and_drains_inflight_work(monkeypatch):
    probe = module()
    monkeypatch.setattr(probe, 'rss_mib', lambda: 2049)
    result = asyncio.run(probe.probe(slots=4, requests=20, wait_ms=5000,
        phase_threads=2, max_rss_mib=2048, warmup=0))
    assert result['stop_reason'] == 'process_rss_limit'
    assert result['completed'] < result['requested']
    assert result['database_removed'] is True
    assert result['drained'] is True


def test_standalone_audit_guard_allows_only_local_workload():
    import json
    import subprocess
    import sys
    result = subprocess.run([sys.executable, str(SCRIPT), '--slots', '2',
        '--requests', '3', '--wait-ms', '10', '--phase-threads', '1',
        '--warmup', '0', '--min-available-mib', '0', '--burst'],
        text=True, capture_output=True, timeout=30)
    assert result.returncode == 0, result.stderr
    evidence = json.loads(result.stdout)
    assert evidence['completed'] == 3
    assert evidence['external_requests'] == 0


def test_decoded_image_buffers_are_closed_before_provider_wait(monkeypatch):
    probe = module()
    # Isolate this image lifecycle contract from cumulative pytest process RSS.
    monkeypatch.setattr(probe, 'rss_mib', lambda: 128)
    monkeypatch.setattr(probe, 'available_memory_mib', lambda: 4096)
    original_open, original_wait = probe.Image.open, probe.ProviderWait
    opened = []
    def track_open(*args, **kwargs):
        image = original_open(*args, **kwargs)
        opened.append(image)
        return image
    def verify_wait(*args, **kwargs):
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match='closed image'):
            opened[-1].getpixel((0, 0))
        return original_wait(*args, **kwargs)
    monkeypatch.setattr(probe.Image, 'open', track_open)
    monkeypatch.setattr(probe, 'ProviderWait', verify_wait)
    result = asyncio.run(probe.probe(slots=1, requests=1, wait_ms=10,
        phase_threads=1, max_rss_mib=2048, warmup=0))
    assert result['stop_reason'] is None, result
    assert result['completed'] == 1, result

===== FILE backend/tests/test_native_capacity_acceptance.py =====
import importlib.util
from pathlib import Path

import pytest


@pytest.mark.parametrize('script', ['probe-native-workflow-capacity.py', 'probe-native-mysql-capacity.py'])
@pytest.mark.parametrize('field,bad', [
    ('timed_out', True), ('provider_live', 1), ('actual_input_tokens', 799),
    ('actual_output_tokens', 119), ('actual_total_tokens', 919),
    ('synthetic_cost_micros', 1039), ('reserved_input_tokens', 47999),
    ('reserved_output_tokens', 32767), ('reserved_cost_micros', 113535),
    ('invalid_invocation_accounting', 1), ('aggregate_completed', 3),
    ('processed_steps', 15), ('runtime_connection_live', 1),
])
def test_native_acceptance_rejects_incomplete_or_misaccounted_work(script, field, bad):
    path = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / 'scripts' / script
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('capacity_gate', path)
    module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    spec.loader.exec_module(module)
    report = dict(
        items=4, runs={'succeeded': 4}, attempts={'succeeded': 16},
        invocations={'succeeded': 4}, provider_requests=4, settled_media=4,
        duplicate_invocations=0, unreleased_quota_leases=0, external_connect_attempts=0,
        transactions_surviving_entire_provider_wait=0, lease_heartbeat_errors=0,
        worker_errors=[], timed_out=False, provider_live=0, actual_input_tokens=800,
        actual_output_tokens=120, actual_total_tokens=920, synthetic_cost_micros=1040,
        reserved_input_tokens=48000, reserved_output_tokens=32768, reserved_cost_micros=113536,
        invalid_invocation_accounting=0, aggregate_completed=4, aggregate_status='completed',
        processed_steps=16, runtime_connection_live=0,
    )
    assert module.acceptance_passed(report)
    report[field] = bad
    assert not module.acceptance_passed(report)


@pytest.mark.parametrize('field,bad', [('baseline_run_count', 1), ('source_types', {'baseline': 2}),
    ('queue_classes', {'validation': 2}), ('reserved_output_tokens', 32768),
    ('processed_steps', 8), ('provider_requests', 2)])

    spec.loader.exec_module(module)

        invocations={'succeeded': 4}, provider_requests=4, settled_media=4, processed_steps=22,
        actual_input_tokens=800, actual_output_tokens=120, actual_total_tokens=920,
        synthetic_cost_micros=1040, reserved_input_tokens=48000, reserved_output_tokens=8192,

        duplicate_invocations=0, unreleased_quota_leases=0, external_connect_attempts=0,
        transactions_surviving_entire_provider_wait=0, lease_heartbeat_errors=0, worker_errors=[],
        timed_out=False, provider_live=0, invalid_invocation_accounting=0, runtime_connection_live=0)
    assert module.acceptance_passed(report)
    assert not module.acceptance_passed(dict(report, **{field: bad}))

===== FILE backend/tests/test_native_multiprocess_capacity_probe.py =====
import importlib.util
from pathlib import Path

import pytest
import subprocess
import sys


def probe_module():
    path = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / 'scripts/probe-native-multiprocess-capacity.py'
    assert path.exists(), 'multiprocess harness is not implemented'
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('multiprocess_probe', path)
    module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    spec.loader.exec_module(module)
    return module


def test_process_evidence_requires_distinct_successful_workers_and_shared_quota():
    module = probe_module()
    reports = [dict(pid=101+i, processed_steps=8, worker_errors=[], timed_out=False)
               for i in range(3)]
    assert module.process_evidence_passed(reports, 3, 24, 20, 0)
    assert not module.process_evidence_passed(reports, 3, 24, 25, 0)
    assert not module.process_evidence_passed(reports, 3, 24, 20, 1)
    reports[1]['pid'] = reports[0]['pid']
    assert not module.process_evidence_passed(reports, 3, 24, 20, 0)


@pytest.mark.parametrize('change', [dict(processed_steps=0), dict(timed_out=True),
                                   dict(worker_errors=['failed'])])
def test_partial_or_failed_process_cannot_pass(change):
    module = probe_module()
    reports = [dict(pid=i+1, processed_steps=8, worker_errors=[], timed_out=False)
               for i in range(3)]
    reports[1].update(change)
    assert not module.process_evidence_passed(reports, 3, 24, 20, 0)


def test_process_network_guard_refuses_external_destinations():
    module = probe_module()
    counts = dict(external_connect_attempts=0, loopback_connect_attempts=0)
    guard = module.network_guard(counts)
    guard('socket.connect', (None, ('127.0.0.1', 3306)))
    for address in [('192.0.2.1', 443), '/tmp/mysql.sock', ('example.com', 443)]:
        with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match='non-loopback'):
            guard('socket.connect', (None, address))
    assert counts == dict(external_connect_attempts=3, loopback_connect_attempts=1)


def test_cli_help_explains_shared_process_envelope():
    path = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / 'scripts/probe-native-multiprocess-capacity.py'
    result = subprocess.run([sys.executable, str(path), '--help'], capture_output=True, text=True)
    assert result.returncode == 0
    assert '--slots-per-process' in result.stdout
    assert '--processes' in result.stdout


@pytest.mark.parametrize('failure', ['worker_error', 'timeout', 'exit'])
def test_first_child_failure_terminates_other_children_immediately(monkeypatch, failure):
    import argparse
    import asyncio
    import queue
    module = probe_module()
    children = []
    events = []

    class Child:
        def __init__(self, **kwargs):
            self.pid = len(children) + 101
            self.exitcode = 7 if failure == 'exit' and not children else None
            children.append(self)
        def start(self):
            pass
        def is_alive(self):
            return self.exitcode is None
        def terminate(self):
            events.append(('terminate', self.pid))
            self.exitcode = -15
        def kill(self):
            self.exitcode = -9
        def join(self, timeout):
            assert not self.is_alive(), 'failed probe waited before terminating live siblings'

    class Output:
        calls = 0
        def get_nowait(self):
            self.calls += 1
            assert self.calls <= 1, 'probe continued polling after the first child failure'
            if failure == 'exit':
                raise queue.Empty
            return dict(pid=101, processed_steps=0, timed_out=failure == 'timeout',
                        worker_errors=['broken'] if failure == 'worker_error' else [])
        def close(self):
            events.append(('closed', 0))

    class Context:
        def Queue(self):
            return Output()
        def Array(self, *args):
            return [0, 0]
        def Barrier(self, *args):
            return None
        Process = Child

    monkeypatch.setenv('DATA_DIR', '/tmp/synthetic-probe')
    monkeypatch.setattr(module.multiprocessing, 'get_context', lambda _: Context())
    result = asyncio.run(module.exercise_processes(argparse.Namespace(timeout=600, slots=24), 3, 8))
    assert not result['multiprocess_passed']
    assert all(child.exitcode is not None for child in children)
    assert sum(event[0] == 'terminate' for event in events) == (2 if failure == 'exit' else 3)
    if failure == 'exit':
        assert any('7' in error for error in result['worker_errors'])
    assert result['provider_peak'] is None


def test_abandoned_shared_counter_lock_never_blocks_or_reads_array():
    module = probe_module()
    assert hasattr(module, 'snapshot_provider_counts'), 'bounded snapshot missing'
    class Lock:
        def acquire(self, *, timeout):
            assert 0 < timeout <= 1
            return False
    class Counts:
        def get_lock(self):
            return Lock()
        def __getitem__(self, key):
            pytest.fail('must never touch synchronized array after failed acquire')
    assert module.snapshot_provider_counts(Counts()) is None


def _report_then_finish_child(index, arguments, root, slots, barrier, counts, output):
    """Real process/queue teardown, without the external MySQL workload."""
    import os
    import time
    barrier.wait(timeout=10)
    with counts.get_lock():
        counts[1] = 3
    output.put(dict(pid=os.getpid(), processed_steps=1, provider_requests=1,
                    worker_errors=[], timed_out=False))
    # A report can arrive before interpreter/thread/queue teardown completes.
    time.sleep(arguments['finish_delay'])
    if index == 0 and arguments.get('late_failure'):
        raise SystemExit(7)


def test_successful_reports_allow_children_to_finish_normally(monkeypatch):
    import argparse
    import asyncio
    module = probe_module()
    monkeypatch.setenv('DATA_DIR', '/tmp/synthetic-probe')
    monkeypatch.setattr(module, 'child_main', _report_then_finish_child)
    result = asyncio.run(module.exercise_processes(
        argparse.Namespace(timeout=15, slots=24, finish_delay=3), 3, 8))
    assert result['multiprocess_passed'], result
    assert result['worker_errors'] == []
    assert (result['provider_live'], result['provider_peak']) == (0, 3)


@pytest.mark.parametrize('failure', ['late_exit', 'stalled_exit'])
def test_success_reports_do_not_hide_failed_or_stalled_exit(monkeypatch, failure):
    import argparse
    import asyncio
    module = probe_module()
    monkeypatch.setenv('DATA_DIR', '/tmp/synthetic-probe')
    monkeypatch.setattr(module, 'child_main', _report_then_finish_child)
    monkeypatch.setattr(module, 'CHILD_EXIT_GRACE_SECONDS',
                        5 if failure == 'late_exit' else .25)
    result = asyncio.run(module.exercise_processes(
        argparse.Namespace(timeout=15, slots=24,
                           finish_delay=.1 if failure == 'late_exit' else 3,
                           late_failure=failure == 'late_exit'), 3, 8))
    assert not result['multiprocess_passed']
    assert result['worker_errors']
    assert result['provider_peak'] is None
    assert all(code is not None for code in result['child_exit_codes'].values())
    if failure == 'late_exit':
        assert 7 in result['child_exit_codes'].values()
    else:
        assert result['forced_termination_pids']
        assert any('exit grace' in error for error in result['worker_errors'])

===== FILE backend/tests/test_native_mysql_capacity_probe.py =====
"""The isolated MySQL probe must reject other databases before DBAPI connects."""
import importlib.util
from pathlib import Path

import pytest


def probe_module():
    path = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / 'scripts/probe-native-mysql-capacity.py'
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('native_mysql_probe', path)
    module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    spec.loader.exec_module(module)
    return module


def test_production_poll_mode_matches_worker_bounded_backoff():
    module=probe_module()
    from app.worker_polling import IdlePollSchedule
    schedule=IdlePollSchedule()
    for idle in (1,2,3,4,100):
        expected=min(4.0,max(1.0,schedule.delay_seconds(idle_count=idle,worker_id='probe-1')))
        assert module.probe_idle_delay(idle,'probe-1',production=True)==expected
        assert module.probe_idle_delay(idle,'probe-1',production=False)==.02


def test_phase_profiler_preserves_result_and_reports_function_cost():
    module=probe_module()
    def work(value):return sum(range(value))
    wrapped,collect=module.profile_local_phases(work)
    assert wrapped(100)==4950
    rows=collect()
    assert any(row['function'].endswith(':work') and row['calls']==1 for row in rows)
    assert all(row['cumulative_seconds']>=0 for row in rows)


@pytest.mark.parametrize('change', [
    {'host': '192.0.2.1'}, {'database': 'production'}, {'user': 'app'},
    {'port': 3307}, {'password': 'not-empty'}, {'unix_socket': '/tmp/mysql.sock'},
    {'read_default_file': '/tmp/config'}, {'init_command': 'USE production'},
])
def test_guard_rejects_nonfixture_mysql_targets_before_connect(tmp_path, change):
    module = probe_module()
    options = dict(host='127.0.0.1', port=3306, user='root', database='native_capacity_probe')
    options.update(change)
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match='isolated'):
        module.validate_database_connect('mysql', [], options, tmp_path, enabled=True)


def test_guard_requires_explicit_mode_and_accepts_exact_fixture(tmp_path):
    module = probe_module()
    options = dict(host='127.0.0.1', port=3306, user='root', database='native_capacity_probe')
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError):
        module.validate_database_connect('mysql', [], options, tmp_path, enabled=False)
    module.validate_database_connect('mysql', [], options, tmp_path, enabled=True)
    module.validate_database_connect('sqlite', [str(tmp_path / 'bootstrap.db')], {}, tmp_path, enabled=True)
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError):
        module.validate_database_connect('sqlite', ['/not-temporary/production.db'], {}, tmp_path, enabled=True)


@pytest.mark.parametrize('objects', [1, 3])
def test_schema_ownership_check_is_read_only_and_refuses_existing_objects(objects):
    module = probe_module()
    class Connection:
        def __init__(self):
            self.queries = []
        def exec_driver_sql(self, query):
            self.queries.append(query)
            assert query.lstrip().startswith('SELECT')
            return self
        def scalar_one(self):
            return 'native_capacity_probe' if len(self.queries) == 1 else objects
    connection = Connection()
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match='empty'):
        module.assert_empty_fixture_schema(connection)
    assert len(connection.queries) == 2

===== FILE backend/tests/test_native_source_run_discovery.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.audit import canonical_json
from app.database import Base
from app.models import (
    Asset,
    BaselineRegressionItem,
    BaselineRegressionRun,
    BaselineSet,
    BaselineSetItem,
    WorkflowDefinition,
    WorkflowModelInvocation,
    WorkflowVersion,
)
from app.native_source_run_discovery import (
    SourceRunDiscoveryError,
    discover_latest_usable_run,
    redacted_source_run_payload,
)
from app.workflow_registry import persisted_workflow_manifest


def _workflow(
    db: Session,
    *,
    category_key: str,
    version: str,
    schema_version: str = "workflow-v3",
    status: str = "active",
) -> WorkflowVersion:
    definition = WorkflowDefinition(
        workflow_key=f"{category_key}-{version}",
        name=f"{category_key} {version}",
        description="",
        owner="ops",
        allowed_categories_json=canonical_json([category_key]),
        status="active",
        created_by="ops",
    )
    db.add(definition)
    db.flush()
    nodes = [
        {
            "node_instance_id": "read_asset",
            "kind": "upstream_connector",
            "config": {
                "source_uri": "nas://must-never-leak/source.jpg",
                "authorization": "must-never-leak",
            },
        },
        {
            "node_instance_id": "prompt_a",
            "kind": "prompt",
            "config": {
                "prompt_version_id": 17,
                "max_tokens": 4096,
                "thinking_level": "high",
                "component_snapshot": {
                    "version": "1.1",
                    "content_hash": "a" * 64,
                    "content": {"system_prompt": "must-never-leak"},
                },
                "model_snapshot": {
                    "model_registry_id": 8,
                    "provider": "doubao",
                    "model_id": "doubao-2.0-pro",
                    "snapshot_hash": "c" * 64,
                    "runtime_config": {
                        "max_tokens": 4096,
                        "thinking_level": "high",
                        "base_url": "https://must-never-leak.invalid",
                        "api_path": "/must-never-leak",
                        "api_token": "must-never-leak",
                    },
                },
            },
        },
        {
            "node_instance_id": "redline",
            "kind": "rule_module",
            "config": {
                "rule_module_version_id": 9,
                "module_type_key": "redline_filter",
                "component_snapshot": {
                    "version": "v9",
                    "content_hash": "b" * 64,
                    "content": {"rules": ["must-never-leak"]},
                },
            },
        },
    ]
    resource_policy = {
        "queue_class": "validation",
        "category_key": category_key,
        "lane_key": "stock_regression",
        "run_modes": ["stock_regression"],
        "max_parallel": 1,
    }
    row = WorkflowVersion(
        workflow_definition_id=definition.id,
        version=version,
        status=status,
        workflow_schema_version=schema_version,
        step_manifest_json=canonical_json(nodes),
        edge_manifest_json="[]",
        input_schema_json=canonical_json({"type": "object"}),
        output_schema_json=canonical_json({"type": "object"}),
        resource_policy_json=canonical_json(resource_policy),
        canonical_hash=("d" if version.startswith("candidate-") else "e") * 64,
        validation_report_json=canonical_json({"ok": True}),
        created_by="ops",
    )
    db.add(row)
    db.flush()
    return row


def _run(
    db: Session,
    *,
    baseline_set: BaselineSet,
    sequence_no: int,
    workflow: WorkflowVersion | None,
    status: str,
    valid_predictions: int,
    created_at: datetime,
    execution_engine: str = "native_workflow",
    frozen_hash: str | None = None,
    model_execution_mode: str = "real",
) -> BaselineRegressionRun:
    execution_snapshot = {}
    if workflow is not None:
        execution_snapshot = {
            "model_execution_mode": model_execution_mode,
            "workflow_snapshot": {
                "workflow": {
                    "workflow_version_id": workflow.id,
                    "version": workflow.version,
                    "canonical_hash": frozen_hash or workflow.canonical_hash,
                    "manifest": persisted_workflow_manifest(workflow),
                }
            }
        }
    row = BaselineRegressionRun(
        baseline_set_id=baseline_set.id,
        sequence_no=sequence_no,
        workflow_version_id=workflow.id if workflow is not None else None,
        execution_engine=execution_engine,
        category_key=baseline_set.category_key,
        strategy_snapshot_json="{}",
        execution_snapshot_json=canonical_json(execution_snapshot),
        baseline_set_fingerprint=baseline_set.fingerprint,
        status=status,
        total=2,
        completed=valid_predictions,
        valid_predictions=valid_predictions,
        failed=2 - valid_predictions,
        metrics_json="{}",
        created_by="ops",
        created_at=created_at,
    )
    db.add(row)
    db.flush()
    return row


def _seed_source_history(db: Session) -> tuple[BaselineRegressionRun, list[int]]:
    category_key = "inspiration_image"
    stable_workflow = _workflow(
        db, category_key=category_key, version="native-r141"
    )
    candidate_workflow = _workflow(
        db, category_key=category_key, version="candidate-correction-10"
    )
    legacy_workflow = _workflow(
        db,
        category_key=category_key,
        version="legacy-v2",
        schema_version="workflow-v2",
    )
    baseline_set = BaselineSet(
        category_key=category_key,
        name="灵感图 50 条验收集",
        description="",
        default_expected_level="L1",
        fingerprint="f" * 64,
        created_by="ops",
    )
    db.add(baseline_set)
    db.flush()

    epoch = datetime(2026, 8, 29, 8, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    stable_run = _run(
        db,
        baseline_set=baseline_set,
        sequence_no=1,
        workflow=stable_workflow,
        status="partial_failed",
        valid_predictions=1,
        created_at=epoch,
    )
    _run(
        db,
        baseline_set=baseline_set,
        sequence_no=2,
        workflow=legacy_workflow,
        status="completed",
        valid_predictions=2,
        created_at=epoch + timedelta(minutes=1),
    )
    _run(
        db,
        baseline_set=baseline_set,
        sequence_no=3,
        workflow=stable_workflow,
        status="failed",
        valid_predictions=0,
        created_at=epoch + timedelta(minutes=2),
    )
    _run(
        db,
        baseline_set=baseline_set,
        sequence_no=4,
        workflow=stable_workflow,
        status="running",
        valid_predictions=1,
        created_at=epoch + timedelta(minutes=3),
    )
    _run(
        db,
        baseline_set=baseline_set,
        sequence_no=5,
        workflow=candidate_workflow,
        status="completed",
        valid_predictions=2,
        created_at=epoch + timedelta(minutes=4),
    )
    simulated_run = _run(
        db,
        baseline_set=baseline_set,
        sequence_no=7,
        workflow=stable_workflow,
        status="completed",
        valid_predictions=2,
        created_at=epoch + timedelta(minutes=6),
        model_execution_mode="simulated",
    )
    _run(
        db,
        baseline_set=baseline_set,
        sequence_no=6,
        workflow=stable_workflow,
        status="completed",
        valid_predictions=2,
        created_at=epoch + timedelta(minutes=5),
        frozen_hash="0" * 64,
    )

    item_ids: list[int] = []
    for index, item_status in enumerate(("completed", "failed"), start=1):
        asset = Asset(
            original_name=f"acceptance-{index}.jpg",
            stored_name=f"acceptance-{index}.jpg",
            mime_type="image/jpeg",
            size_bytes=100,
            sha256=str(index) * 64,
            category_key=category_key,
        )
        db.add(asset)
        db.flush()
        set_item = BaselineSetItem(
            baseline_set_id=baseline_set.id,
            asset_id=asset.id,
            expected_level="L1",
            expected_result_json=None,
            truth_contract_key="level.v1",
            asset_snapshot_json=canonical_json(
                {"asset_id": asset.id, "source_uri": "must-never-leak"}
            ),
        )
        db.add(set_item)
        db.flush()
        item_ids.append(set_item.id)
        db.add(
            BaselineRegressionItem(
                run_id=stable_run.id,
                baseline_set_item_id=set_item.id,
                asset_id=asset.id,
                expected_level="L1",
                expected_result_json=None,
                truth_contract_key="level.v1",
                status=item_status,
                result_snapshot_json=canonical_json(
                    {"raw_response": "must-never-leak"}
                ),
                error_message="must-never-leak" if item_status == "failed" else "",
            )
        )
        db.add(
            BaselineRegressionItem(
                run_id=simulated_run.id,
                baseline_set_item_id=set_item.id,
                asset_id=asset.id,
                expected_level="L1",
                expected_result_json=None,
                truth_contract_key="level.v1",
                status="completed",
                result_snapshot_json="{}",
                error_message="",
            )
        )
    db.add(
        WorkflowModelInvocation(
            baseline_run_id=stable_run.id,
            production_run_id=90_001,
            step_attempt_id=90_001,
            node_instance_id="prompt_a",
            status="succeeded",
            provider="doubao",
            model_id="doubao-2.0-pro",
            reserved_input_tokens=1,
            reserved_output_tokens=1,
            input_tokens=1,
            output_tokens=1,
            total_tokens=2,
            estimated_cost_micros=1,
            evidence_json="{}",
        )
    )
    db.flush()
    return stable_run, item_ids


def _walk_keys(value: object) -> list[str]:
    if isinstance(value, dict):
        return [str(key) for key in value] + [
            nested
            for child in value.values()
            for nested in _walk_keys(child)
        ]
    if isinstance(value, list):
        return [nested for child in value for nested in _walk_keys(child)]
    return []


def test_discovery_skips_newer_unusable_and_candidate_runs() -> None:
    """Catches accidentally treating latest row as latest stable mechanism."""

    engine = create_engine("sqlite:///:memory:")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            stable_run, item_ids = _seed_source_history(db)

            evidence = discover_latest_usable_run(
                db, category_key="inspiration_image"
            )

            assert evidence.run_id == stable_run.id
            assert evidence.workflow_version == "native-r141"
            assert evidence.item_ids == tuple(item_ids)
            assert evidence.failed_item_ids == (item_ids[1],)
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_discovery_returns_only_redacted_version_evidence() -> None:
    """Catches leaking prompt bodies, connector locations, or provider secrets."""

    engine = create_engine("sqlite:///:memory:")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            _seed_source_history(db)
            payload = redacted_source_run_payload(
                discover_latest_usable_run(
                    db, category_key="inspiration_image"
                )
            )

            prompt = next(
                item for item in payload["components"] if item["kind"] == "prompt"
            )
            assert prompt == {
                "node_instance_id": "prompt_a",
                "kind": "prompt",
                "prompt_version_id": 17,
                "component_version": "1.1",
                "component_snapshot_hash": "a" * 64,
                "model_registry_id": 8,
                "provider": "doubao",
                "model_id": "doubao-2.0-pro",
                "max_tokens": 4096,
                "thinking_level": "none",
                "thinking_enabled": False,
                "model_snapshot_hash": "c" * 64,
            }
            rule = next(
                item
                for item in payload["components"]
                if item["kind"] == "rule_module"
            )
            assert rule == {
                "node_instance_id": "redline",
                "kind": "rule_module",
                "rule_module_version_id": 9,
                "module_type_key": "redline_filter",
                "component_version": "v9",
                "component_snapshot_hash": "b" * 64,
            }
            assert {key.lower() for key in _walk_keys(payload)}.isdisjoint(
                {
                    "token",
                    "api_token",
                    "password",
                    "secret",
                    "authorization",
                    "cookie",
                    "dsn",
                    "base_url",
                    "api_path",
                    "source_uri",
                }
            )
            assert "must-never-leak" not in json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False)
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_discovery_rejects_unknown_category() -> None:
    """Catches silently falling back to another category's paid source run."""

    engine = create_engine("sqlite:///:memory:")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            _seed_source_history(db)

            with pytest.raises(SourceRunDiscoveryError) as exc_info:
                discover_latest_usable_run(db, category_key="model_3d_su")

            assert exc_info.value.code == "source_run_not_found"
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_discovery_can_skip_a_newer_one_item_canary() -> None:
    """Catches replacing a 50-item source pool with the latest one-item smoke run."""

    engine = create_engine("sqlite:///:memory:")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            stable_run, _item_ids = _seed_source_history(db)
            workflow = db.get(WorkflowVersion, stable_run.workflow_version_id)
            assert workflow is not None
            canary = _run(
                db,
                baseline_set=db.get(BaselineSet, stable_run.baseline_set_id),
                sequence_no=8,
                workflow=workflow,
                status="completed",
                valid_predictions=1,
                created_at=datetime(2026, 8, 29, 9, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc),
            )
            canary.total = 1
            canary.completed = 1
            canary.failed = 0
            db.flush()

            evidence = discover_latest_usable_run(
                db,
                category_key="inspiration_image",
                minimum_items=2,
            )

            assert evidence.run_id == stable_run.id
    finally:
        engine.dispose()
